the pre-implementation audit of 2026-09-10 (`references/audit/2026-09-10/MASTER.json`, 110 concerns, and its grounding census); the S-MIG census at corpus pin `47faec9f`; the external research pass of 2026-09-08 (48 confirmations, 13 contradictions, 44 new findings, 30 unresolved); Claude Code, Claude platform, gitoxide, crates.io and competitor documentation as fetched 2026-09-08. · **Evidence discipline:** every load-bearing claim carries a finding number or a source; where a claim rests on one draw, one machine or one operator, it says so. Claims are tagged **[measured]** / **[documented]** / **[inferred]**. A saturated metric is reported as saturated, a powerless criterion as powerless, and a ground truth known to be wrong is never used as a denominator. Every unknown is discharged as a named spike with a named consumer (ADR-0012), never as prose.

---

## 0. The design in one paragraph

Build Redkiln-RS as a **clean-break, single Rust binary that owns the loop**: a **deterministic kernel** — stage selection, gate evaluation, budgets, retry, replay — that holds no non-determinism of its own and dispatches **one harness process per activity**, every one of them a child with its own card, schema, tool set, MCP config and cost record; durable state written as **git trailers first and an append-only event log second**, split by the test *if the database were deleted, would `redkiln run` take a different next action?*; **human gates as durable marks** — a `(token, tree_hash, deadline)` record, a `Gate:` trailer and a process that exits 0, never a process that waits; and **every agent output a typed value** validated at the boundary, of which markdown is a rendering and never a source. The kernel is hexagonal along five named variation axes — event store, harness, tracker, docs site, toolchain — each a **sync** trait with one real adapter and one fake (two reals for `Toolchain` — Node and Rust — ADR-0094), with every `.await` confined to an adapter behind a cancel token the composition root owns (ADR-0076, ADR-0099); git is deliberately not a port. What separates this revision from the 2026-09-05 proposal at revision 4 is that the P-series and the research pass moved the line between built and blocked in **both** directions: P6 collapsed the lattice's unsatisfiability from 23.5% to 0.3% and left zero rank ties and zero hard cycles (F35) while P9/P9b/P6 showed the activity-graph schema is authorable at a median 53–73 non-blank lines against a >150 kill (F32/F33/F34), so the catalog, the schema and the selection engine's *semantics* are now decided and buildable; and against that, P1's kill fired 4 of 4 on the selection engine's *input* (F36) and arm D's scoring then found the **derived** half of that input measurably wrong rather than merely unstable — no facet clears METHOD-P §1.4's 0.95 bar, `auth` scores **0.00** — 0/12 [0.00, 0.24] after the panel-file key fix (E-0089-2), eleven-of-eleven false before it — and the bar itself is unreachable at n=20 (F39; ADR-0089; ADR-0093 places the four kernel projections and the declared `surfaces` map outside the derived gate) — P7's primary criterion proved powerless at n=3 rather than negative (F37), P8 returned no result on its own question (F38), and the research pass withdrew two facts the proposal leaned on — gix's `GIT_COMMON_DIR`/`GIT_WORK_TREE` interplay and its trailer-write capability (ADR-0077) and the single-variable TTL fix (ADR-0078). The design is therefore decided in more places than the proposal was, and final in fewer.

---

## 0.1 What is decided, and what is blocked

Thirty-nine areas. **Twenty-six DECIDED, three PROVISIONAL, ten BLOCKED** — counted off the rows below, which is where the split had drifted: the header read 25/4 after the E3 false-positive row moved from PROVISIONAL to DECIDED on 2026-09-09 and the tally did not move with it. The ninth blocked row is new as of 2026-09-09: F39 scored the *derived* facet source, which AC-071's block never covered, and ADR-0089 records what follows. **The tenth is new as of 2026-09-10:** F40 scored P2's perturbation half, and step 3's `stale_policy` is blocked not because a criterion stayed silent but because the one criterion that *fired* prescribes a remedy the same measurement contraindicates. Blocked rows are listed first because they are the ones a reader must not miss; nothing later in this document resolves one of them in prose.

| Area | State | The evidence | What unblocks it | Gates Phase 1? |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Selection engine — the context-vector source** | **BLOCKED** (ADR-0079, status `superseded` — superseded in full by **ADR-0090**, accepted, at runbook Step 4.4; corrected 2026-09-15, this read *"status `proposed` — the only non-accepted record in the set other than two full supersessions"*, and both halves are false — see §4.1) | P1's kill fired **4 of 4**: the modal `R(stage, ctx)` was identical across ten draws on **5/20, 3/20, 2/20 and 2/20** items against an 80% line; the prescribed anchor-rewrite repair did not work; `external_dep` collapsed to a constant (**200/200 false**); three axes with **byte-identical** anchors drifted up to **−0.168**, larger than the effect the alpha<0.6 rule exists to detect, so that rule is **not a decidable predicate on this corpus**; `unknown` rates novelty 46.0%, size 39.5%, blast_radius 31.0%, criticality 27.0%. [measured, F36; 400 dispatches, 0 void, $21.37] **Arm D is now SCORED** and its answer is not a threshold miss: over **140 truth cells, all scored** at pin `47faec9f` (the 2026-09-09 run scored 136; the panel-file key `resolve-open-questions` → `resolve-open-question` is fixed and `score-arm-d-panel.py` fails closed on a truth cell with no engine record — ERRATA E-0089-2), **no derived facet clears METHOD-P §1.4's 0.95 bar** — `ui` **1.00** [0.65, 1.00] recall 0.78, `toolchain` 0.73 [0.48, 0.89], `change` 0.72 [0.54, 0.85], `binary_change` 0.60 [0.23, 0.88], `flag_exists` 0.00 [0.00, 0.66], **`auth` 0/12 [0.00, 0.24], every positive false** (0.00 [0.00, 0.26] over eleven before the key fix), and `data_migration` **NOT MEASURABLE** (0 positives across all 20 items and all three sources — the criterion has no denominator; the expected count at a base rate ≈ 1.7% of revisions, Qiu, Li & Su ESEC/FSE 2013, E-0089-1). The ≥ 0.95 clause is **not reachable at n = 20 for any facet however good**: `ui` is 7/7 and its lower bound is 0.65, and **73** all-correct positives are needed for a lower bound above 0.95 at the registered two-sided z = 1.96 (92 with continuity correction; 72 Clopper–Pearson — not the ~60 first stated, E-0089-2) — the same class of defect as P1's alpha rule (F36) and P7's criterion 1 (F37) [measured, F39; 140 cells, all scored after the key fix, **$0** model cost, **panel-adjudicated** under METHOD-P amendment 9, never human-panel]. **F39 discharges the fourth of AC-071's five clauses and no other** — the arm-D panel is scored — and **the block still stands on the first**: F36 measured modal required-set identity at **0.25 / 0.15 / 0.10 / 0.10** against the 0.80 line. F39 measures a *derived* vector; ADR-0079 asks about an *LLM-emitted* one, so it narrows 0079's options without deciding among them and **ADR-0079 is not amended into a decision by F39** (corrected 2026-09-15; this read *"`proposed`"* — ADR-0079 is superseded in full by **ADR-0090**, and the correction is carried in place and dated, not by an erratum: see §4.1 and **DECISIONS-2026-09-15 D15-2**). Externally consonant: instability at temperature 0 is reported across five frontier models by arXiv 2603.04417 (GPT-4o, GPT-4o-mini, Gemini-2.5-Flash, Claude-Haiku-4.5, Claude-Sonnet-**4.5**; "limited or inconsistent effects for Anthropic models") and for Claude-Sonnet-**4** by arXiv 2604.22411; **neither paper states a schema-induced mechanism** — 2603.04417 points at decoding-level differences — so "schema-induced, not decoding noise" is this design's own reading [inferred, F36 + ADR-0079; documented, full texts fetched 2026-09-10; amended 2026-09-11, PIA-020] | **AC-071's five clauses, in full and in one place**: identity ≥ 0.80, n ≥ 20 items, ≥ 10 draws per item, arm-D panel scored, corpus pinned. **The fourth is DISCHARGED** — the panel was adjudicated and arm D scored on 2026-09-09 (136 cells) and re-scored over all 140 after the key fix, at $0 model cost [measured, F39; E-0089-2] — and clauses 2, 3 and 5 were met by arm L's own record (20 items × 10 draws at a pin). **What remains is clause 1 alone:** a P1 re-run whose modal `R(stage, ctx)` identity reaches **0.80**, against the **0.25 / 0.15 / 0.10 / 0.10** measured [F36] — §4.9 and §10.2 exit (6) state the same condition and no other. A temperature spike is explicitly not the answer | **YES** — Phase 1 may not author a `require` row keyed on `criticality`, `novelty`, `size`, `blast_radius`, `market_facing`, `external_dep` or `has_repro` sourced from an LLM classification dispatch. `kind` is out of scope of the block (human input, ADR-0030 rule 4). The engine's **semantics** are decided and buildable now |
| **Selection engine — the DERIVED-facet source** | **BLOCKED** (**ADR-0089**, accepted 2026-09-09; it partially supersedes ADR-0030, which stays `accepted` and now carries `superseded_by: ["0089"]`) | **AC-071's block reaches `source = "llm"` only, so the semantic facets — declared `source = "derived"` — ran outside it entirely, and they are the ones measurably wrong.** The deriver is an unanchored, case-insensitive regex over file paths **and diff lines**. Scored against **140 truth cells, all scored** at pin `47faec9f` (136 on 2026-09-09; the panel-file key fix, E-0089-2): **`auth` 0/12 [0.00, 0.24] — every positive false (eleven of eleven before the key fix), so the rule carries NO SIGNAL on this corpus**; `flag_exists` 0.00 [0.00, 0.66]; `binary_change` 0.60 [0.23, 0.88] recall 1.00; `change` 0.72 [0.54, 0.85]; `toolchain` 0.73 [0.48, 0.89]; `ui` 1.00 [0.65, 1.00]; `data_migration` **NOT MEASURABLE**. The collision is **correlated, not random** — it is with the vocabulary of the instrument under test: `auth` fired 43 times, **40 of them inside *authored* / *author* / *authors* / *authoring* / *report-md-unauthored* / *only-the-author-can-advance***; `token` 44 times, **36 inside a design token** (`brand-token-substrate`, `brand-tokens.test.ts`, `redkiln.tokens.json`, `tokens_out`); `oauth` 8 times, **all 8 inside *coauthor*** (the string `oauth` sits inside `c-oauth-or`); `session` 8, six of them a *work* session; `role` 2, both an ARIA attribute. And **4 of the 20 items changed no code whatsoever** — every changed file is `.bklg/` bookkeeping or telemetry — the panel returned `unknown` on **all 16** of their semantic-facet cells and the script emitted a confident boolean on every one. [measured, F39; **$0** model cost, reported **PANEL-ADJUDICATED** under METHOD-P amendment 9 and never as human-panel — 60 cells a deterministic oracle over git metadata, 39 unanimous panel, 9 unanimous with the adversarial flag mechanically moot, and **32 human adjudication, Ryan blind, of which 15 of 32 differ from the script**] | **S-FACET** (§10.5), pre-registered in `references/2026-09-09-change-classification.md` §6 with four arms — control, anchored-paths-only, `surfaces.toml` declared boundaries, declared + call-site — and a kill criterion whose either outcome is a legitimate result. Named consumer: `facets.toml`'s `source`/`deriver` fields. **Source asymmetry is a separate, untaken decision registered as an arm of it**: `commits` beats `docs` on every facet (`toolchain` **1.00** [0.70, 1.00] vs **0.50** [0.22, 0.78]; `change` **0.80** [0.61, 0.91] vs **0.50** [0.24, 0.76]; `binary_change` finds nothing at all from `docs` — recall 0.00, 3 missed) and **`union` is worse than `commits` alone** on `toolchain` and `change`, because unioning a precise source with an imprecise one imports the imprecision [measured, F39 §3]. ADR-0089 does not decide it and does not touch ADR-0079 | **YES** — no `require` row or `applicable_if` may be keyed on `auth`, `ui`, `flag_exists` or `data_migration` as computed by the lexical deriver (`applicable_if` is evaluated over the item's context vector at selection and never re-evaluated inside the activity — ADR-0103 decision 6 — so the block reaches both forms alike; §8.10's row says the same). ADR-0089 withdraws the repo-wide `auth` boolean outright and replaces it with a **set-valued `surfaces` facet** over a checked-in `surfaces.toml` path-glob map (set-valued is precedented by `toolchain` and `change`), which is **unscored until S-FACET runs** and may therefore be authored and linted but may **not** key a `require` row on ADR-0089's strength alone. **No** for the engine, whose semantics are settled. Two of ADR-0089's three parts ship in Phase 1: `facets.toml`'s `source` gains a companion **`deriver`** field and no facet may declare `source = "derived"` without a record for that deriver — **six** derived facets at the pin, each with a record (NOT MEASURABLE included), while `stage`/`rigor`/`role`/`cost` are `source = "kernel"` and `surfaces` is `source = "declared"`, neither gated by AC-520 (**ADR-0093**, partially superseding ADR-0089; the eleven-derived count and the checkpoint's magic eleven are withdrawn); and **boolean facets become tri-valued — absence of evidence is `null`, never `false`** — routed to AC-079's existing `gate_opened { reason: unknown_facet }`, which ADR-0067's `recent_deploy` already does under `R-recent-deploy-feed-absent`, so the pattern is generalised rather than invented |
| **Selection step 3 — the `stale_policy` setting** | **BLOCKED** (no ADR carries it; it is registered by **AC-068**, PROVISIONAL, and **SM-021**, which has no target) | **P2's perturbation half is SCORED, and its answer does not set the policy.** 41 `(case, downstream)` rows over 12 perturbation cases at pin `47faec9f`, **$0** model cost, labelled by Ryan Britton and **blind and timestamp-verified** — `labelled_at` **2026-09-10T15:58:08Z** precedes the engine's earliest record at **16:01:12Z**, checked by the scorer's own guard, and P2 is unaffected by METHOD-P amendment 8, so its chronology proxy has not expired. **P2 is the only arm in this programme whose blindness is *checked* rather than attested**; arm D is panel-adjudicated and its amendment-8 attestation route was never invoked, and the two may not be blurred. **0 of 41 rows are "should re-run" — the label set has no positive class**, and that is the corpus and not the labeller: classifying every hunk against each artifact's own YAML frontmatter boundary at the pin, **11 of the 12 cases change nothing but frontmatter** (`stage`, `status`, `updated`, `gate_open` — the keys the redkiln CLI rewrites on every stage advance) and the twelfth (P01) changes **one body line** — a single rewritten line, which the unified diff shows as a `-`/`+` pair of wording; the labeller had written "only frontmatter properties (process/stage status), no new/updated facts of the system" against **36 of the 41 rows** (35 the phrase exactly, one a shorter variant of it; four rows carry no reason at all and one is P01's) before any of that was computed. The mechanism is a recorded decision: DECISIONS-2026-09-09 **#19** doubled the case count from 6 to 12 by adding the **card** as a perturbation candidate, and cards are exactly what the CLI churns — the 11 frontmatter-only cases are the 11 card perturbations. **So recall has no denominator, and METHOD-P §2.4's criterion 1 (recall half) and criterion 2 in full are NOT ANSWERABLE** — the fourth undecidable pre-registration in this programme after F36, F37 and F39. Criterion 1's **precision half FIRES** — 0.00 [0.00, 0.26] over 11 emitted positives — but precision is not the honest statistic here: with no positive labels every `Stale` is a false positive **by construction**, so 0.00 is a property of the label set, not a reading of the engine. What the corpus genuinely measures is **specificity: coarse 0.73 [0.58, 0.84], fine 0.55 [0.38, 0.70]**. Those intervals **overlap, so the aggregate does not separate the two scopes**; the paired comparison over the **33 rows both scopes scored** does — coarse marks **5** `Stale`, fine marks **15**, and **10 rows flip `Present` → `Stale` going coarse to fine while none flips the other way**. Fine's abstention rate is **8 of 41 = 19.5%** [0.10, 0.34] — a reconstructed scope of `unknown`, excluded from scoring and **never counted as a miss** (DECISIONS-2026-09-09 #16) *(#16 is cited for the RULE — an `unknown` reconstructed scope is an abstention, never a miss — not for its number: #16's own measured figure is **15 of 77** over the reconstruct records and rounds to the same 19.5% by coincidence. The two are never interchangeable.)*. **The remedy criterion 1 prescribes — fine scope mandatory for `L`-class — is therefore CONTRAINDICATED by the same measurement.** The mechanism is F39's failure class one level up: the fine scope *reconstructs* `derived_from` as "which upstream changed before this artifact was last written", and so fires on `implementation-report.md` and `report.md`, which the declared map does not connect to the card at all, because the CLI rewrites cards so often that a card is nearly always the most recent prior write — an inference over a trace the instrument itself writes into, exactly as F39's `auth` regex collided with the vocabulary of a tool whose documents are *authored*. **Criterion 3, the advisory half (A01–A06), is STILL UNRUN** [measured, F40; 41 rows, 12 cases, **$0** model cost, timestamp-verified] | Two things, and neither is a default: **P2-B**, a re-run on a corpus carrying **real content perturbations** — the only thing that can give recall a denominator, since no item in this corpus carries a substantive upstream change — and **P2's advisory half** (A01–A06, criterion 3), which is labelled after the engine in its own file and behind DECISIONS-2026-09-09 **#15**'s `human_authored` remap. Until both report, the setting is taken by an explicit decision or not at all (§10.5) — **and it has been, on 2026-09-11** (DECISIONS-2026-09-11 #4): the TOML site is `policy.<cost_class>.stale_policy`, the enum `rematerialise \| gate`, and the Phase-1 value is **`rematerialise` with coarse scope for every class**, recorded per class with its warrant at Step 7e, never a code default; Checkpoint 8 asserts no value was defaulted from P2 (§4.4 step 3). P2-B and the advisory half own any evidence-based change | **No** — the step-3 **mechanism** ships and is unit-tested (AC-066, AC-067; ADR-0025), and ADR-0025's own "[unmeasured] The advisory's actual noise rate is untested" stands untouched, because the advisory half is what is unrun. **No `stale_policy` default may be set from P2's run, and no policy may make the fine scope mandatory on the strength of criterion 1 firing** |
| **`ChangeSummary` type design** | **BLOCKED** (fixture pinned 2026-09-09; spike still unrun) | **S-BIN registered and UNRUN — but no longer unplanned.** [S-BIN reported (s8i) 2026-10-14: run 2026-10-14, ten records valid at corpus `835b122`; F43 and ADR-0199, both proposed *(dated 2026-10-15, `s8i` close, D21-154: F43 and ADR-0199 accepted)*, fix the `Binary` arm's four fields and route an item carrying a `Binary` path to the human gate; this row's state moves when ADR-0199 is accepted] The fixture is pinned and its five items and two seeds are registered (METHOD-S §7, 2026-09-09), and reading the real project already found three places the draft type does not fit what it emits: under LFS `--numstat` reports an asset change as a **pointer-line diff** — `1 1` or `2 2` — and never the binary marker, so `text_diff` cannot be decided by asking git; `tests` has no field for `succeededWithWarnings`, which is **180 of 1029** on the project's own last run; and `captures` has two candidate producers — the manual `/ue-verify` route (17 PNGs at the pin, in use) and UE's automation comparison (never run) [amended 2026-09-11, PIA-070]. Those are inputs to the spike and **fix nothing about the type** [measured, METHOD-S §7 amendment 2026-09-09]. **The seeds' greenness, which §3.2 requires verified before dispatch, has NOT been run and may retire either seed.** No review evidence of any kind exists for items whose change is not a text diff [documented; ADR-0008, ADR-0012] | S-BIN on the real Unreal project: 5 items whose change is asset manifest + automation report + captures, no diff; can the reviewer verify ACs and catch a seeded regression | **YES**, explicitly — for the `ChangeSummary` type and the Toolchain port's `change_summary` body, exactly as ADR-0008's "(type design)" words it (ADR-0093 decision 4); it blocks no other Phase-1 commit, and `ChangeSummary` sits as an opaque non-finalised newtype in `rk-ports` meanwhile (ADR-0099) *(amended 2026-09-24, `s8a`, ADR-0165: no longer so. `ChangeSummary` is an enum defined only in `rk-ports`, with a `Text` arm finalised now and a `Binary` slot whose payload is sealed until S-BIN reports at `s8i`; S-BIN now gates the `Binary` arm and its body only)*. The five toolchain-keyed kinds (ADR-0073) cannot be finalised without it |
| **Card-delivery append route** | **BLOCKED** | **S-H1c registered and UNRUN**; ADR-0016's delivery clause is conditional on it [documented; ADR-0016, ADR-0078] | S-H1c — measure the append route against the static `--system-prompt` baseline | **No** for the Phase-1 default: static `--system-prompt` ships (ADR-0016, untouched by ADR-0078's partial supersession). **The append route may not be enabled as anything but an unmeasured config option until S-H1c runs** |
| **The quality shorthand default** | **BLOCKED** (ADR-0080 leaves it explicitly unset) | P7's primary criterion is **powerless at n=3** — every Wilson interval overlaps by construction (3/3 → [0.439, 1.000] against 0/3 → [0.000, 0.561]; at n = 3 no two Wilson intervals can be disjoint, so §4.4(1) could not have failed to fire — ADR-0111, PIA-073). The `adversary → policy` block cost **2.64×** ($2.69 vs $1.02/run) and its policy check was **false on 33 of 33**. The frequently-quoted "changed the root cause on 1 item in 10" is **the powerless criterion's own output, not a result** — one cell (`issue-81`, 0/3 vs 1/3) moved by one draw of three, with nothing excluding 0 and nothing excluding much more (F37 §2); deviation was offered 71 times and used 0. [measured, F37; 61 runs, 297 records, 0 void, $131.62] The evidence half works: 188 findings, **78% required running something**. **Instrument confound, carried with every P7 figure:** the CLI moved from 2.1.263 to **2.1.265 partway through P7**, catching exactly `recommended`'s r3 draw on all ten items while `optional` ran wholly on 2.1.263 — no effect is detectable in F37 §7's per-repeat split, and **the arms are still not instrument-matched** | **S-P7B** — a re-run under **a changed criterion, not only a larger n** (ADR-0111): a paired per-item comparison, or an interval on the pooled difference of proportions with a stated minimum detectable effect and the n that reaches it (≈ 20 per cell for 0 vs 0.33 under interval overlap; n = 10 per cell is powerless) — plus a settled severity vocabulary *(2026-09-26, ADR-0168 proposed: the severity vocabulary is settled as `blocking \| advisory`; no admissible severity threshold T exists on this evidence, and ADR-0080 decision 2 bullet 2 governs)*. **R-P7RATIO** — the mandatory arm's cost ratio, 3.66× in F37 against 4.4× in the decisions log, is **reconciled by denominator** (ADR-0106 clause 3): 4.4× = $9.857/$2.222 (issue-77 r1, single run), 3.66× = $9.857/$2.693 (arm mean, n = 30) [amended 2026-09-11, PIA-110, PIA-073] | **No** — Phase 1 authors each quality block explicitly per activity. **No default may be set from the P7 run**, and the forbidden predicate `findings.none(evidence in ["Reproduced", "MutationSurvived"])` may not be used as a terminal condition (ADR-0080 decisions 1–4; ADR-0111) |

| Cache rule is a split rule, scoped to machine and directory | **DECIDED** (verification of it is the BLOCKED row above) | The two mitigations point in **opposite** directions and this may not be collapsed: `--system-prompt` holds share **1.000** across commits [F16, 50 dispatches, within-arm range ≤0.001] and — on a prompt attribution the record does not establish — across worktrees w2–w10 [S-WT: w1 0.807, w2–w10 all 1.000 — carried without a finding number in the brief; F30 was drafted 2026-09-06 and promoted 2026-09-11 to `spikes/F30-DRAFT.md`; **the configuration is recovered from `spikes/results/s-wt.jsonl`**: one cardless one-line `--system-prompt`, never combined with `--agent`, n = 9 steady-state worktrees, so S-WT and F16's worktree-each 0.685 are two cells (static × worktree; default × worktree), not a contradiction — §7.7, AC-387; PIA-075, PIA-066], but **forfeits the shared-prefix subsidy across time** — not an account-wide or org-wide one: **§7.2 records that there is no account-wide shared head**, and the brief's "account-wide" wording is retired here for the same reason "org-scoped" was — creating **29,680** tokens at share **0.000** at 66.8 min [F28, **n=1**] and **29,619** at share **0.000** at 66.1 min [S-H1b, **n=1**, and S-H1b carries **no finding number**] — **two single draws from two spikes, quoted separately and never as an interval** — where the default prompt costs a flat 18,603 at both 65.2 min and 49.7 h, one draw per point [F28 correction, S-H1b]. Scope is **workspace-scoped** on the API/AWS/Foundry, org-scoped only on Bedrock/GCP, and additionally **machine + directory + git-status** scoped inside Claude Code — the proposal's "org-scoped" is withdrawn. Two worktrees of one repo therefore **never** share a cache; every worktree pays a cold first turn, structurally. [documented, 1P, fetched 2026-09-08] ADR-0078 | — | Yes |

Per CLEAN draw is **0.0098** five_hour (60 draws, four rows pooled; 0.0102 over the three-row pool of 56) — **F21's 0.077 is 7.8× too high and is superseded** [F29]. The binding constraint on a CLEAN set is **wall clock, not headroom** (16.2 h for 60 draws). The delta is over readings sharing one `resetsAt`, in integer ticks, `low_sample` below n = 10, `not attributable` on a shared account — **ADR-0110** (the rule's home; ADR-0003 never contained it, PIA-082, PIA-061) | — | Yes |

The adapter parses `SDKRateLimitEvent` and tolerates both shapes with a named error on neither | — | Yes |

Rigor is `forbidden < discouraged < optional < recommended < mandatory` — ordinal, never summed, because **rank reversal occurs for virtually every weighted MCDM method**, TOPSIS and SAW included [peer, Wang & Luo 2009, S0895717708002860 (SAW and TOPSIS), and Aires & Ferreira 2018, as quoted by arXiv 2312.03006; García-Cascales & Lamata 2012, S0895717711007850, is TOPSIS-only and its full text was not read — re-pinned 2026-09-11, PIA-098]. Authoring is tractable: 90 activities at a median **53** non-blank lines, **0.11** first-run lint errors per file against P9's 4.7 — PC5 clears by **2.8×** [F34]; the schema authors at a median 56.5 and a second author at 73 (+29%), against a >150 kill, **but P9's "amendments will fall" was falsified at n=3** [F32, F33]. SQLite serialises writers, so one writer connection per process behind a mutex, never a pool — the docs.rs read is now taken: rusqlite 0.40.2's `busy_timeout` default is 5000 ms and `Connection: Send + !Sync` [documented, docs.rs, fetched 2026-09-10; ADR-0105 clause 7]; **R-SQLITE**'s two-process contention test (§9.4) is what remains. The pin is **rustc 1.98.1** (DECISIONS-2026-09-11 #7; one recorded instrument move from the spike programme's 1.97 line), `rust-version` equals it, member `[lints]` is `workspace = true` only, `#[expect]` is the only in-source override (ADR-0102). ADR-0009, ADR-0010, ADR-0011, ADR-0094 | — | Yes |

**How to read the ratio.** Twenty-six of thirty-nine areas are decided on measurement, and the spine of the build — kernel, ports, store, git substrate, the two gates that compute their blocks in code, the cost model — is among them. Three are provisional and say what would settle them. Ten are blocked, and three of those ten (the selection engine's LLM-emitted input, its derived-facet source, and `ChangeSummary`) stop specific Phase-1 work rather than the phase. **The method finding this table exists to honour:** five metrics saturated in this programme, and every discriminating result came from **cost, stability or mechanism — never from success rates** (brief C.12, F28). A row that reads "decided" here is decided on that kind of evidence or it is not decided at all.

---

## 1. What the evidence settled, and what it did not

Three tables. **1.1** is what the F-series closed and what each closure licenses a later section to assume. **1.2** is the register of everything that did not close — saturated, powerless, struck, not answerable, unrun or contested — with a Hedge column, because a reader who takes 1.1 without 1.2 will over-read this document. **1.3** is the short list of results that changed the thesis since `2026-09-05-architecture-proposal.md` rev. 4 was written; those five are the reason a fresh document exists rather than an errata sheet. §0.1 carries the decided/blocked ledger by area — this section carries the evidence behind it and does not repeat it.

### 1.1 Settled

| Question | Answer | Evidence | What it licenses |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Does moving review out of the interactive session into headless dispatch degrade it? | **No degradation is detectable — and the instrument could not have detected one.** Detection **23/23 vs 23/23** (no discordant pairs, so no test on detection; **McNemar exact p = 0.625 is on the CLEAN false-positive 3-vs-1 discordant pairs**, E-0001-1); cost **$97.39 vs $56.47 = 1.72×**; `parent_inspected` **0/46**. CLEAN false positives settle to **1/23 vs 0/23** and MCC **+0.957** (tp 23 / fn 0 / fp 1 / tn 22) vs **+1.000** after the severity adjudication closed 2026-09-09 — on a sheet dated 2026-09-04, filled by the model at Ryan's direction, **NOT BLIND**, κ forfeited (§1.2; E3-SEV-BLIND is the open route) — and both are computed against a ground truth wrong for **≥13 of 23**, so the FP figures are still **upper bounds** and an MCC against that key is not a settled figure [amended 2026-09-11, PIA-081, PIA-073]. Re-run at two harder tiers: **20/20**, sighted and blind (5/5 ×4), $36.18, blind +18% median wall — **all four cells at ceiling, so this is a second saturated pair and confirms nothing about a between-arm difference** | [measured, F23; measured, F26] | ADR-0001/ADR-0002 — the clean-break single binary owning the loop, on the ground that **no difference was detectable and the instrument could not have detected one** — never "nothing was lost", which is a non-inferiority claim a tie at ceiling with no margin and no power cannot support, and never that headless wins on quality. **The primary metric saturated at ceiling in both arms, so it ranked nothing**; the only discriminating figure in E3 is cost. Any later section citing F23 must cite it as a non-inferiority result with a saturated primary |
| Are model and reasoning effort pinnable per dispatch? | **Yes, and the pin is a role property, materialised per dispatch.** `--agents` is **INERT without `--agent`** (canary: both → BANANA; `--agents` alone → 4); the bound card's model + effort are accepted. Effort **is** applied per dispatch: thinking tokens low **0.0** / high **4.8** / xhigh **221.0**, complete separation; output 121/130/360 — **no measured step between low and high on sonnet at n=5, one prompt; xhigh is the only measured step** | [measured, F7, which retires RFC R2; measured, F15 — sonnet, n=5, one prompt, subscription tier] | Per-activity model/effort resolved from the role's tier into the **bound** card by the binder and refused at configuration time when incoherent (AC-203, AC-212, AC-236, ADR-0003); the authored card file may not carry `model`/`effort` (AC-118, AC-418, ADR-0014) — the two rows agree once "resolved card" is the term [amended 2026-09-11, PIA-015]. On this evidence a pack that spends on `high` expecting more thinking than `low` is spending on nothing measured here; the opus/harder-task contrast is unmeasured and would need a registered spike with a consumer before any stronger statement (PIA-076) |
| What does the prompt cache actually do under commits and under worktrees? | **Steady-state cache-read share: no-commit 1.000 · commit-each 0.689 · +`--exclude-dynamic` 0.846 · +`--system-prompt` 1.000 · worktree-each 0.685.** A commit costs ~31% of the prefix, half of it recoverable. The prefix has **two segments** — ~17,439 shared across directories + ~7,827 per-directory, 19/19 fresh repos identical — so a new worktree costs ~31%, not 100%. Across worktrees the S-WT record reads w1 0.807, **w2–w10 all 1.000** — under **one cardless one-line `--system-prompt`, never combined with `--agent`** (`spikes/results/s-wt.jsonl`; METHOD-S §4b.2), so the hold is attributable to the static prompt and S-WT and F16's worktree-each arm are two cells, not a contradiction (§7.7, AC-387) [amended 2026-09-11, PIA-075, PIA-066] | [measured, F16; 50 dispatches, within-arm range ≤0.001] · [measured, F13] · [measured, **S-WT** — `spikes/results/s-wt.jsonl`, n = 9 steady-state worktrees, haiku, `--strict-mcp-config`, an 18-byte fixture tree, one draw per worktree; carried without a finding number in the brief, F30 drafted 2026-09-06 and promoted 2026-09-11 to `spikes/F30-DRAFT.md`] | `cache_profile` as a per-activity setting (AC-383, SM-113, ADR-0078). **Worktree cold-start is priced per cell and never interpolated:** AC-387 prices static × worktree from S-WT (w2–w10 = 1.000, n = 9; w1 = 0.807 cold) and default × worktree from F16 arm (d) (0.685, n = 9), returns `Unmeasured(cell)` for cells no spike ran — the card + `--system-prompt` × worktree cell among them — and never interpolates between two separately-measured arms, which differ by roughly 31× on a fan-out. **Forbids** the inference that "held across commits" implies "helps across time" — explicitly falsified by the F28 correction (brief C.1); see §7, which owns the two-mitigations-point-in-opposite-directions rule |
| Is suspension lossless, and at what granularity? | **Lossless at both granularities measured.** Activity granularity across a process boundary: **174/174**. Mid-graph step granularity across a real `taskkill /T /F`: **3/3 — 0 changed, 0 lost, digests identical** | [measured, F19] · [measured, F38; **n=3, PARTIAL**] | The workflow/activity split, the memo table and mid-graph gate resume (AC-013, AC-161, AC-185, AC-187, ADR-0021, ADR-0022, ADR-0086, ADR-0087). **Stated limit, carried in every citation:** the FSM was **never replayed**, so "same next action" is unproven and ships as a kernel replay test (AC-006, AC-188), and P8's state holds exactly one step (brief C.4) |
| Does a human gate get more expensive the longer it stays open? | **No — gate cost is flat in duration.** A gate costs a bounded ~**18,603** tokens at **65.2 min** and **18,603** at **49.7 h** — identical to the token, at **one measured point per duration (n=1 each)**, so duration-invariance is two draws and not an established law. At 0.9 min, share 1.000 / creation 0. Under `--system-prompt` at 66.8 min the same gate creates **29,680** at share **0.000** | [measured, F28, **with the published correction that the flatness was shared-prefix subsidy, not durability**] | Gate duration is free, frequency is marginally costly, and **placement is therefore a value decision, not a cost decision** (brief C.2; ADR-0009, ADR-0017, ADR-0087; SM-073). It does **not** license calling the gate durable — the mechanism is a subsidy that `--system-prompt` forfeits |
| Is a structured digest sufficient to carry a gate across a suspension? | **Measured sufficient, with no variance to detect insufficiency in.** H4 Jaccard **FULL 1.000 = CONTROL 1.000, effect +0.000** — the digest carries what the next step uses | [measured, F28] · **zero-variance caveat:** both arms sat at 1.000, so this is a saturated pair, not a demonstrated equivalence. The research pass declines the strong form outright: no first-party or peer-reviewed source affirms "prose adds nothing", and the vendor's own compaction page says that after compaction "full tool outputs and intermediate reasoning are gone" while compaction preserves important code and decisions — the nearest live first-party text; the "nuanced understanding…" quotation the research pass carried is on no live first-party page [documented, code.claude.com/docs/en/context-window and /how-claude-code-works, fetched 2026-09-10; research pass §1 row 6, severity medium; amended 2026-09-11, PIA-099] | A `files[]` + evidence digest as the resume payload, **with rationale and caveats carried as explicit structured fields (`notes` / `caveats`), never as discardable prose** (AC-037, research pass §4 rec 18). Forbids stating digest sufficiency for judgment-bearing content |
| Does the affected-area verification gate catch a real regression? | **It kills 9 of 10 test-reachable mutants.** 15 mutants; 9 killed at test, **1 survived**; headline **93.3%**, test-reachable **90.0%**; median wall **323 s** | [measured, F11] | The affected-area gate as the norm and mutation as per-AC evidence (AC-289, AC-340, SM-101, ADR-0019). **The CI on 1 of 10 is wide and is reported as wide** — this is a floor for "the gate does something", not a precision figure. **Scope, carried with every F11 citation: 8 of 23 corpus items sit outside S0.1 entirely, because their fixes touch only `workflows/` and not `src/` — a third of the corpus is unmeasured by this instrument, by construction** (§1.2, **S0.1-SCOPE**). |
| Is `Story:`-trailer resume alone enough to reconstruct a slice's history? | **No — 1 slice in 5 passed through a rejected state.** 360 trailer commits over 74 slices; **ever-rejected 15/74 = 20.3%**; sealed-more-than-once **21.6%** | [measured, F12; **the registered primary was uncomputable at n=1** — 1 of 101 runs recorded the field — so the finding is re-scoped and disclosed as such] | The second trailer axis (`Slice-Verdict:`) and the tier-1/tier-2 split (ADR-0018, AC-162, AC-165, AC-371; §5 owns the mechanics). A single-axis trailer design would lose a fifth of the state it exists to hold |
| Is the reviewer's *verdict* usable as a gate signal? | **No — the verdict is a weighted coin and the findings are not.** Detection **0/7 items flaky over 21 draws**; **verdict 2/4 items flaky**. Across 60 draws: **not one defect class is stable-reject** (class means Reproduced **0.70** > MutationSurvived 0.37 > NarrowedPin 0.10 > control **0.00**) while **19/19 rejections were on-target** | [measured, F27; n=2–3, and a "stable" label at this n is compatible with a true flip rate up to ~56% (n=3) / ~66% (n=2), Wilson 95% — not the "~30%" first stated, PIA-073] · [measured, F29; 60 draws, 0 void, $176.96, 16.2 h] | Reviewer-proposes / machine-confirms: the evidence class is computed in Rust and a finding blocks only after the deterministic gate re-executes it and it flips (ADR-0074, full supersession of ADR-0004; AC-285, AC-286, SM-087, SM-091). Externally consonant: coding items sit at 23.6% instability in the high-flip stratum and a single trial matches a 50-trial majority only 86.6% of the time [preprint, arXiv 2606.13685] |
| Does forcing the reviewer to emit a typed value cost detection? | **No, and conformance was perfect.** 51 valid records, **schema conformance 52/52 = 100%**, `repro.command` fill **147/147**, detection unchanged (S-SILENT 23/23, S-SUBTLE 5/5 — both saturated), **verified false positives 0/23** — a **floor** result and an **upper bound against a ground truth wrong for ≥13 of 23** — adjudication **14/14 confirmed**, at **+74%** cost ($112.25 vs prose $64.61) and **1.6×** median wall (1,071 s vs 682 s) | [measured, F31] | Typed artifacts with markdown as a rendering, and nothing parsing structure out of prose (ADR-0005, ADR-0006, ADR-0075; AC-280, AC-281, SM-039, SM-040). Conformance is a **ceiling result** — it proves the contract is satisfiable, not that it is hard to satisfy |
| Can the catalog and its lattice actually be authored and closed? | **Yes, on both counts.** Authoring: 90 activities at a median **53** non-blank lines, sum 5,059, **0.11** first-run lint errors per file against P9b's 1.3 and P9's 4.7 — PC5 clears by **2.8×**; the schema itself authors at a median **56.5**, and a second author at **73 (+29%)**, against a **>150** kill. Lattice: unsatisfiability **23.5% → 11.4% → 0.3%**, dead activities **15/101 → 6/102**, **0 rank ties**, **0 hard cycles**, 27 s runtime | [measured, F34; F32; F33 — all zero model dispatches] · [measured, F35] | The v0 catalog, the activity-graph schema and the lattice CI (ADR-0027, ADR-0028, ADR-0030, ADR-0031–ADR-0073; AC-100–AC-103, AC-106, AC-112, SM-025–SM-027, SM-030, SM-031). **Two disclosures travel with it:** F35's pre-registered reading **fires the kill twice** (23.5%, 11.4%) and only the amended post-decision lattice clears all three criteria — both readings are reported; and F33 **falsified P9's "amendments will fall" at n=3** (27 ambiguities, 20 of them new) |
| Can a tool-restricted card be trusted to return a schema? | **No — it fails open, silently.** An agent card's `tools` allowlist disables `--json-schema`: `structured_output` absent, **exit 0**, `subtype: success`; adding `StructuredOutput` fixes it | [measured, F20] | `StructuredOutput` required in every card whose role demands a schema, checked at **configuration** time (brief C.5, AC-234) |
| Is a default `claude -p` a controlled measurement environment? | **No.** A default child inherits the operator's whole environment: 12 MCP servers, ~280 tools; cache write **8,243 → 3,611** isolated; MCP alone was **4,631 tokens = 56%** of the write; tools 280 → 34 | [measured, F8] | Isolation as a measurement precondition, **decided from the child's own `system/init` and never from argv, because an ignored flag looks identical to an honoured one** (brief C.11, AC-202, SM-114) |
| May anything depend on a process surviving? | **No.** ~12 kills against one **12,000 s** survivor under `Start-Process`; `issue-81` was killed 3× as a background job and completed in **116 s** in the foreground | [measured, operational, F22; **cause still unestablished**] | Every wait is a durable mark plus a later read; no waiting process anywhere in the design (brief C.6, ADR-0017) |
| How often does the world move under an open gate? | **Rarely, and predictably.** Drift **12/174 = 7%** overall, and **3.6× more likely** past 1 h (9/79 = 11% vs 3/95 = 3%; RR 3.6 with CI [1.0, 12.9], Fisher p = 0.039 — **directional, marginal at n=174, one operator**, never an established ratio, PIA-073), while **45–48% of gates exceed 1 h** (gate→verdict n=174 median 10.5 min; stage→verdict n=191 median 51.6 min, p90 ~9 h, **max 226 h = 9.4 days**). Median in-gap commits is 0 | [measured, F19; F18 — zero model cost, **one operator, one machine**] | `reconcile()` at every entry, and **no optimisation that skips it** (brief C.3; ADR-0018; AC-126, AC-172, AC-173, AC-176, SM-049, SM-058) |

### 1.2 Not settled, saturated, struck, or known wrong

Every row here is a place where a number exists but does not mean what it appears to mean, or where no number exists at all. The Hedge column names the branch taken meanwhile; the discharge is §10's spike register (ADR-0012). Rows marked **†** are among the **ten broken pre-registrations** the brief records in §E. **The ninth postdates the brief's 2026-09-08 condensation and was added to its §E list on 2026-09-09, taking that list from eight to nine:** METHOD-P §1.4's precision ≥ 0.95 rule is **not reachable at n = 20** for any facet however good, which F39 reports in its own Limits as a result *about* the pre-registration and never as an amendment to it — the same class as P1's alpha rule and P7's criterion 1, both already on this list. **A tenth was added on 2026-09-10:** METHOD-P §2.4's criteria 1 and 2 cannot be decided by P2's own registered sample either — that corpus produced **no positive labels at all**, so recall has no denominator — which F40 likewise reports in its own Limits as a result *about* the pre-registration and never as an amendment to METHOD-P. **Four of the ten are now one kind of defect**: a threshold fixed without checking that the registered corpus could produce the denominator the threshold needs (F36, F37, F39, F40). Four is a pattern in how this programme writes pre-registrations, not four coincidences.

| Item | State | Why it cannot be read as a result | Evidence | Hedge |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

What actually remains untracked is 17 items: the raw P1/P7/P8 driver logs, `spikes/results/p8-state/`, and the `spikes/runner/target-dev/` and `target-p8/` build directories (`.gitignore` covers `target` but not those two names). The F36/F37/F38 drafts, `spikes/DECISIONS-2026-09-09.md` and every `spikes/results/*.jsonl` are tracked. **Measured 2026-09-10 at HEAD `009a4ec`: 0 ` M`, 17 `??`** — the same 17 untracked items; everything the figures rest on is tracked [measured, PIA-019; the earlier "nothing from the AFK session was committed" residual is deleted as stale] | [documented, brief §E; measured, PIA-019] | Every figure above traces to `spikes/` files at that state; the S-MIG census is separately pinned at `47faec9f` |

### 1.3 The five results that changed the thesis

These are the results that postdate `2026-09-05-architecture-proposal.md` rev. 4 and move a claim it made. Every other correction in this document is a refinement; these five are reversals.

| Result | What rev. 4 held | What now holds | Evidence | Consequence for this document |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **F36** — P1's kill fires 4 of 4 on the selection engine's *input* | The selection engine could be driven by an LLM-emitted context vector, with a per-facet alpha≥0.6 check as its acceptance gate | The modal `R(stage, ctx)` was identical across ten draws on only **5/20, 3/20, 2/20 and 2/20** items against an **80%** line; the prescribed anchor-rewrite repair **did not work**; `external_dep` collapsed to a **constant (200/200 false)**; three axes with **byte-identical anchors** drifted up to **−0.168**, so the alpha rule is not a decidable predicate here; `unknown` rates novelty 46.0%, size 39.5%, blast_radius 31.0%, criticality 27.0%. Arm D was left uncomputed by F36 and has since been **scored by F39**: no derived facet clears METHOD-P §1.4's 0.95 bar, `auth` scores **0.00** (0/12 [0.00, 0.24] after the panel-file key fix, E-0089-2), and the ≥ 0.95 clause is unreachable at n=20 for any facet (73 all-correct positives needed) — which discharges **AC-071's fourth clause only**, leaves the block standing on the first (identity 0.25 / 0.15 / 0.10 / 0.10 against 0.80), and opens a **separate** block on the *derived* source under ADR-0089 | [measured, F36; 400 dispatches, 0 void, $21.37; measured, F39, for arm D — 140 cells, all scored after the key fix, $0, panel-adjudicated]. Externally consonant: instability at temperature 0 is reported across five frontier models by arXiv 2603.04417 (Claude-Sonnet-**4.5**, Haiku-4.5, GPT-4o, GPT-4o-mini, Gemini-2.5-Flash) and for Claude-Sonnet-**4** by arXiv 2604.22411; **neither states a schema-induced mechanism** — that reading is this design's own [inferred, F36 + ADR-0079; documented, full texts fetched 2026-09-10; PIA-020] | **BLOCKED (ADR-0079, `superseded` in full by ADR-0090; corrected 2026-09-15, this read *"`proposed` — the only non-accepted record other than two full supersessions"*, and both halves are false — see §4.1).** The engine's **semantics** are decided and buildable; the **source** of its facets is not. §4 states the block at full strength in its own subsection and may not repair it. Phase 1 may not author a `require` row keyed on an LLM-classified facet; `kind` is out of scope of the block (human input, ADR-0030 rule 4). Do not spend a spike on temperature — apply the boundary-distance diagnostic to the existing draws instead [preprint, arXiv 2511.01176; research pass §4 rec 21] |
| **F37** — P7's primary criterion is powerless, not negative | The `adversary → policy` quality block was a recommendable default and the rigor ladder was monotone | The block costs **2.64×** ($2.69 vs $1.02/run) and its policy check was **false on 33 of 33**; the "**1 item in 10**" root-cause change is one cell moved by one draw of three — the powerless criterion's output, named here only to record that it may not be used as an effect; deviation was offered **71 times and used 0**. The criterion is **powerless at n=3** — every Wilson interval overlaps by construction. The evidence half of the block does work: **188 findings, 78% required running something**. And the ladder is **not monotone**: `mandatory` + `has_repro` selects `bisect`, which has no adversary and no policy step | [measured, F37; 61 runs, 297 records, 0 void, $131.62] | **BLOCKED (ADR-0080).** No default may be set from this run, and the predicate `findings.none(evidence in ["Reproduced", "MutationSurvived"])` may not be used as a terminal condition. The criterion's re-run must change the criterion, not only n (ADR-0111). **ADR-0081** makes lattice monotonicity a lint (AC-105, SM-035); the broken rung is repaired by `bisect`'s `max_rigor = "recommended"` ceiling (ADR-0093 decision 5). §4 and §6 author quality blocks explicitly and set nothing by default |
| **F38** — P8 returned no result on its own question | "Design final" was reachable this pass, and mid-graph gate placement was unpriced | **P8 has no result.** 3 draws of a pre-registered 27+3, **$3.01 of a $40 cap**; its **65-minute and 24-hour arms — the ones that straddle F10's ~1 h TTL — and all three no-kill controls are unrun**; its third criterion is **circular by construction**; its §5.2 fixture design **failed outright**, so every gate arrival was forced and the arrival rate is unmeasured. What it did return: mid-graph resume is lossless across a real kill (**0 changed, 0 lost, digests identical**) at a resume cost that is **unmeasured against a stage gate** — the sub-1× output-token ratios first quoted divided the resumed step's `output_tokens` by F28's `cache_creation_input_tokens`; on the like counter the resumed dispatches created 84,960 / 49,468 / 46,961 cache tokens (4.57× / 2.66× / 2.52× of 18,603, cross-fixture) [E-0087-1] — with all three runs inside the TTL and **no within-experiment baseline** | [measured/PARTIAL, F38; n=3; ADR-0087, ADR-0106] | **"Design final" is BLOCKED as a label** (§0.1). Phase 1 starts on the DECIDED rows; **no document, section or commit message may describe this design as final.** ADR-0087 is PROVISIONAL; its cost clause is withdrawn by ADR-0106 and placement is not derived from a cost model |
| **Research contradiction #2 — gix does not implement the `GIT_COMMON_DIR` / `GIT_WORK_TREE` interplay** (severity **high**) | Phase 1's `rk-git` would use gix to manage worktrees for a store living under `git-common-dir` and shared across worktrees | gitoxide's own `crate-status.md` states that "the delicate interplay between `GIT_COMMON_DIR` and `GIT_WORK_TREE` isn't implemented" — **precisely the mechanism the store design leans on**. This is a named gap in first-party documentation, not a hypothesis. Separately (contradiction #8, severity medium): trailer **parsing** exists at the pin — `BodyRef::trailers()` in gix-object 0.64.1, despite `crate-status.md`'s unchecked box — and no first-class trailer-**writing** API comparable to `git interpret-trailers --in-place` exists there, a **[documented] absence** from a docs.rs read of 2026-09-10 (E-0077-1), the executed-test arm of S-GIX/b still owed; named in §5 as "the highest-value single check in the list" because trailers are tier-1 state | [documented, 1P GitoxideLabs/gitoxide `crate-status.md`, fetched 2026-09-08; docs.rs gix-object 0.64.1, fetched 2026-09-10; research pass §1 rows 2 and 8, and §5] | **BLOCKED on the read; the hybrid ships meanwhile (ADR-0077, restated by ADR-0098).** gix owns `rev-parse` — with the common-dir read falling back to `git rev-parse --git-common-dir` through the wrapper on an S-GIX/a no-go — status and trailer *parsing*; the shallow clone by SHA (`PrepareFetch::with_revision` + `with_shallow`, PR #2824 — not discussion #2309, which shows a panic) is a gix **write** retained deliberately; `git worktree add/remove` and `git commit --trailer` (with `-c trailer.ifexists=add`, ADR-0105) shell out. **gix may not own the write path, and §5 may not claim gix can write a trailer**, until **S-GIX/b**'s write half passes against the exactly-pinned `gix = "=0.87.1"` [amended 2026-09-11, PIA-009, PIA-042] |
| **Research contradiction #3 — native async fn in trait is still not dyn-compatible** (severity **high**) | A dyn-dispatched async port needed no `async-trait` under current Rust | Native async-fn-in-trait (stable since 1.75) is **still not dyn-compatible**; the vtable-signature problem is unchanged and `async_fn_in_dyn_trait` **remains nightly-only** — confirmed by a full read of the 1.97.0 and 1.98.0 release notes on 2026-09-10 (tracking issue rust-lang/rust #133119 open; R-ASYNC discharged negative through 1.98.1). "No generics leak into the core" and "dyn-dispatched port" are in **direct tension** the moment a port method is async | [documented, 1P rust-lang.github.io/async-fundamentals-initiative; docs.rs/async-trait; fetched 2026-09-08; research pass §1 row 3] | **ADR-0076, taken explicitly rather than left implied:** port traits are **sync**, every `.await` is confined to an adapter holding its own runtime handle, and cancellation is a token the composition root owns, implemented as a Job Object kill on Windows and a process-group kill on POSIX (ADR-0099). §3 owns the contract. The release-notes read is taken: neither 1.97 nor 1.98 stabilises it, so "still requires `async-trait` for dyn ports" is the working assumption through the 1.98.1 pin, re-checked at every pin bump [documented, fetched 2026-09-10; PIA-024] |

Three of the research pass's thirteen contradictions carry severity **high**, not two: the third is contradiction #1, the TTL bucketing correction, whose consequence is a blocked verification rather than a changed thesis — it appears in §1.2 as charter E1's struck metric and is owned by §7 (ADR-0078, spike **S-TTL1**).

### 1.4 How this ledger is discharged

Nothing in §1.2 is left as a prose hedge. Every row resolves to a named spike with a named consumer in §10's register, which is the mechanism ADR-0012 requires; where the discharge is a human judgement rather than a spike — the P1 arm-D panel (**adjudicated 2026-09-09**; 32 of its cells are Ryan's own verdicts, F39, attested-blind since 2026-09-11 — DECISIONS-2026-09-11 #13), the E3 severity adjudication (**closed 2026-09-09, NOT BLIND**), the `bisect` ceiling question (**decided 2026-09-11**, ADR-0093 decision 5) — the row names the person and the artifact, not an intention. **Two conventions this document uses specialise documented requirements-engineering practice; no agile-AC-specific norm was found** [amended 2026-09-11, PIA-086; ERRATA E-0085-1]. The AC-### + `depends_on` / `tracesTo` convention, under which a criterion names its own verifying instrument, specialises the requirement-grain practice that NASA SP-2016-6105 Rev 2 Appendix D's Requirements Verification Matrix (Table D-1, p. 241; Table 4.2-2, p. 70) and ISO/IEC/IEEE 29148's per-requirement verification-method attributes document — those norms name a method class, not a test id; and the PROVISIONAL / BLOCKED AC phrasing for a deliberately undecided item extends NASA SP-2016-6105 Appendix C's TBD/TBR items (rationale, owner, closure date) [documented, fetched 2026-09-10]. The earlier "no documented norm" and "ahead of any external norm" claims are withdrawn; both conventions are load-bearing for the whole apparatus and both are specialisations, not inventions. Identifier conventions across the four families of design text (ADR-NNNN, AC-###, SM-###, and the F-series) are fixed by ADR-0085, which also records that ADR immutability is this repository's chosen convention rather than an inherited industry norm [1P github.com/adr/madr, fetched 2026-09-08; research pass §1 row 11].

---

## 2. Principles the data imposes

Nineteen principles. The proposal's thirteen are all here, restated against the evidence as it now stands; six are new, forced by the P-series and the research pass. **Two changes of order and content matter enough to state before the table.** First, the method finding is no longer eleventh — it is principle 1, because it is the rule that decides whether any of the others may be called evidenced (brief §C.12). Second, three of the original thirteen rest on different evidence than they did: "nothing waits" now also rests on F38's mid-graph result, "absence of evidence is a block" now rests on F20's measured silent-schema-void mechanism, and "typed in, typed out" has had its strongest clause withdrawn by the research pass. §2.2 carries the full old→new map, so a `principle N` citation written against proposal §3 stays resolvable.

Each principle names the finding that forces it and states what it forbids. A principle that cannot be traced to a finding is not here. Where a principle touches a BLOCKED area of §0.1, the block is stated in the row and not resolved.

### 2.1 The nineteen

| # | Principle — and what it forbids | Forced by | Pinned by |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **1** | **Measure cost, stability and mechanism — not success rates.** *Forbids:* presenting a saturated metric as a win; reporting a powerless criterion as a negative result; reporting a criterion with **no denominator** as either a pass or a failure; reading a pre-registered bar as decided when the registered sample size cannot decide it; using a ground truth known to be wrong as a denominator; quoting a per-run figure the instrument cannot resolve. | Five metrics saturated in this programme and every discriminating result came from cost, stability or mechanism [brief §C.12]. E3's detection was **23/23 vs 23/23** (no discordant pairs, no test; McNemar p = 0.625 is on the CLEAN FP 3-vs-1 pairs, E-0001-1) while the cost separated at **1.72×** ($97.39 vs $56.47) [measured, F23]; 40 dispatches / ~1.0 M tokens (854k cache-read, 157k cache-creation, PIA-077) moved `seven_day` **0.100 → 0.100** [measured, F14]; `utilization` is quantised to **0.01**, so E0's cache-cost gate was **STRUCK, not answered** [measured, F6]; P7's primary criterion is **powerless at n=3**, every Wilson interval overlapping by construction [measured, F37]; CLEAN's ground truth is wrong for **≥13 of 23 = 57%**, so every false-positive rate against it is an **upper bound** [brief §E, contested]. **F39 adds both of the new clauses:** `data_migration` emits **0 positives on 20 items across all three sources**, so precision is undefined and a perfect method scores identically — it is reported **not measurable**, never as a pass or a failure; and METHOD-P §1.4's 0.95 bar is **not reachable at n = 20 for any facet however good**, `ui` at 7/7 buying a 95% lower bound of **0.65** against the **73** all-correct positives a lower bound above 0.95 needs at z = 1.96 (E-0089-2) [measured, F39]. | AC-019, AC-381 (the question the resolution cannot answer is refused, not estimated), AC-382 (`low_sample` refused as an automatic input), AC-394, AC-396; ADR-0012 |
| **2** | **Rust holds the loop; the model holds no control flow.** Every `advance`, gate, retry and budget is code; the agent *classifies* and may *request* a typed deviation. *Forbids:* any model-produced value as an input to selection, review tiering, a budget decision or a terminal condition. | F36 converts this from a preference into a requirement: **400 dispatches, 0 void, $21.37**, and the modal `R(stage, ctx)` was identical across ten draws on only **5/20, 3/20, 2/20 and 2/20** items against an 80% line [measured, F36]. The typed deviation channel was **offered 71 times and used 0** [measured, F37] — the channel is right, its traffic is unmeasured. | AC-001, AC-003 (no `sleep`, no direct clock), AC-302 (`resolve_review_tier` takes no model-produced value), AC-113; ADR-0022, ADR-0030 |
| **3** | **Determinism is a property of the input as well as the engine — and this input is BLOCKED.** A deterministic selector over an unstable vector is not deterministic selection. *Forbids:* authoring a `require` row keyed on `criticality`, `novelty`, `size`, `blast_radius`, `market_facing`, `external_dep` or `has_repro` sourced from an LLM classification dispatch, until a P1 record clears AC-071's **identity ≥ 0.80** line; **and, separately, authoring one — `require` row or `applicable_if` alike — keyed on `auth`, `ui`, `flag_exists` or `data_migration` as computed by the lexical deriver, until S-FACET reports** (ADR-0089; ADR-0093 scopes S-FACET to exactly those rows and `surfaces`, and places `stage`/`rigor`/`role`/`cost` in a `kernel` class a `require` row may key on freely). `kind` is out of scope (human input, ADR-0030 rule 4). | P1's kill fired **4 of 4**; the prescribed anchor-rewrite repair did not work; `external_dep` collapsed to a constant (**200/200 false**); three axes with **byte-identical** anchors drifted up to **−0.168**, larger than the effect the alpha<0.6 rule exists to detect, so **that rule is not a decidable predicate on this corpus**; `unknown` rates novelty 46.0%, size 39.5%, blast_radius 31.0%, criticality 27.0%, and `unknown` is treated as `high` by position, so an axis oscillating unknown↔low moves `R` the full band width [measured, F36; brief §C.9]. Externally consonant with instability at temperature 0 across five frontier models (arXiv 2603.04417, Sonnet-4.5; arXiv 2604.22411, Sonnet-4) — "schema-induced, not decoding noise" is this design's inference, stated by neither paper [inferred, F36 + ADR-0079; PIA-020]. **The principle is now half-discharged and doubled.** Arm D is **scored**: no derived facet clears METHOD-P §1.4's 0.95 bar, `auth` scores **0.00** — 0/12 [0.00, 0.24] after the panel-file key fix, every positive false (E-0089-2) — and the ≥ 0.95 clause is unreachable at n=20 [measured, F39; 140 cells at pin `47faec9f`, **$0**, panel-adjudicated]. That discharges **AC-071's fourth clause only** — the block on the LLM-emitted vector stands on the first, identity **0.25 / 0.15 / 0.10 / 0.10** against 0.80 — and it opens a second, distinct block on the **derived** vector, which AC-071 never covered (**ADR-0089**). | AC-009, AC-113 (**PROVISIONAL/BLOCKED**), AC-302; **ADR-0079** (status `superseded` — ADR-0090 supersedes it in full *because* it forbade being amended into a decision; corrected 2026-09-15, this read *"status `proposed`"* — see §4.1); **ADR-0089** (accepted, for the derived half). The LLM half is unblocked by a P1 record clearing identity ≥ 0.80 and nothing else; the derived half by **S-FACET** — see §0.1 and the register in §10 |
| **4** | **Git is truth; the record is a cache; `reconcile()` runs at every entry that can cause an action** (ADR-0109 decision 1; `status` and `explain` are read-only — ADR-0105 clause 8, corrected 2026-09-12 from "every entry"). *Forbids:* any optimisation that skips reconciliation on an acting entry, and any write from the store back to git. | Drift is **12/174 = 7%** overall and **3.6× more likely** on gates over an hour (**9/79 = 11%** vs **3/95 = 3%**) [measured, F19], while **45–48%** of gates exceed an hour [measured, F18; n=174/191, one operator, one machine]; median in-gap commits is **0** — rare, predictable, cheap to catch. The substrate under this principle moved: gitoxide's own `crate-status.md` states the `GIT_COMMON_DIR`/`GIT_WORK_TREE` interplay "isn't implemented", which is precisely the mechanism the store leans on [documented, 1P GitoxideLabs/gitoxide, fetched 2026-09-08]. | AC-172, AC-173, AC-174, AC-176, AC-183; ADR-0018, **ADR-0077** (hybrid adopted, spike **S-GIX** named) |
| **5** | **Nothing waits.** Human gates, long gaps, rate-limit resets and fan-out joins are a durable mark plus a later read. *Forbids:* depending on a process surviving; a `sleep` in the workflow; treating a live process as a gate. | **~12 kills against one 12,000 s detached survivor**, and `issue-81` killed three times as a background job completed in **116 s** in the foreground, cause unestablished [measured/operational, F22]; a gate costs a bounded **~18,603 tokens** whether it lasts **65.2 min or 49.7 h** — identical to the token, on **one draw per duration point** — with the published correction that the flatness was **shared-prefix subsidy, not durability** [measured, F28]. **New evidence (F38):** the mark/read split now also holds *mid-activity* — mid-graph state survived a real `taskkill /T /F` with **0 changed, 0 lost, digests identical**, at a resume cost that is **unmeasured against a stage gate** — the sub-1× output-token ratios first quoted divided `output_tokens` by F28's `cache_creation_input_tokens` (E-0087-1; ADR-0106) [measured, F38; **n=3, PARTIAL** — see the caution below]. | AC-024, AC-035, AC-185, AC-186, AC-187; ADR-0009, ADR-0017, **ADR-0087** |
| **6** | **Absence of evidence is a block, and the absence is usually silent.** `Result<Findings, StepError>`. *Forbids:* an absent digest read as `pass`; an exhausted budget read as `pass`; an absent usage field read as zero; a green suite read as a cleared claim. | F20 is the measured mechanism: an agent card's `tools` allowlist **silently disables `--json-schema`** — `structured_output` absent, **exit 0**, `subtype: success`; adding `StructuredOutput` fixes it [measured, F20]. The failure looks exactly like success, which is why the check is configuration-time. Reinforced twice: four adjudicated defects escaped the deterministic gate — two through fully green suites (**1,890/1,890** and **1,907/1,907**) and two (issue-131) with zero new failures against an already-red 54-failure baseline [measured, F31; E-0082-2]; and Red→Green is evidenced at *test* granularity while an AC is a claim about *one assertion* — on the AC-015 probe, removing step 7 turned the suite RED while inverting the ordering left **36 of 36 GREEN** [measured, F25; n=1 AC, one operator]. The flat `ephemeral_1h_*`/`ephemeral_5m_*` naming was **measured absent** while the nested `cache_creation` object carrying both is present on 954/954 runs [measured, F3 as re-read; PIA-067]. | AC-007, AC-025, AC-031, AC-282, AC-320, AC-346, AC-362 (`Option` with no `Default`, no `unwrap_or(0)`); ADR-0019 |
| **7** | **Explicit over discovered; adoption and isolation are verified from the child's own report.** Every dispatch passes card, system prompt, tools, schema, MCP config and permission mode. *Forbids:* inferring from the parent side that a flag took effect — an ignored flag looks identical to an honoured one. | `--agents` is **INERT without `--agent`** (canary: both → BANANA; `--agents` alone → 4) [measured, F7]; a default `claude -p` inherits the operator's whole environment — 12 MCP servers, ~280 tools — and `--strict-mcp-config` moved `cache_creation` **8,243 → 3,611**, of which MCP alone was **4,631 tokens = 56%** of the write, tools **280 → 34** [measured, F8]. Isolation is a **measurement precondition**, not hygiene [brief §C.11]. | AC-195, AC-214, AC-215, AC-386 (a run whose isolation flags were not honoured has its rows marked non-comparable and excluded from every cost aggregate), AC-486, AC-511; ADR-0003, ADR-0010 |
| **8** | **Typed in, typed out; markdown is a rendering — but a digest carries files, not judgment.** *Forbids:* parsing structure out of prose; a locator treated as a grammar; and (**changed**) asserting that a structured digest loses nothing — a judgment call that matters downstream is carried as an explicit typed field or it is not carried. | Under the typed contract, schema conformance was **52/52 = 100%** and `repro.command` fill **147/147**, at **+74%** cost [measured, F31]. The type must be *permissive* because the corpus forces it: **19 of 153** shipped specs (12%) write criteria with **no GWT (76 loss lines) or no verification note (87 loss lines)** (the per-AC sub-counts first stated were cap-truncated, E-0075-1) — AC-137's predicate, disjunctive over specs, not a "neither…nor" count — gate results are free-form in **39/156** reports and **19/23** reviews, and `plan.md`'s artifact list was the unfilled scaffold in **110/110** [measured, S-MIG — **no F-number**; 1,312 files / 6,877 records at corpus pin `47faec9f`, zero model cost; the lifters were iterated four times against this corpus, so these are shares under a locator tuned to the corpus, not a blind parse]. **Withdrawn:** "the reviewer's prose adds nothing" has no first-party or peer support, and the vendor's own compaction guidance argues the opposite for judgment-bearing content [documented, 1P code.claude.com context-window / prompt-caching, fetched 2026-09-08; research pass §1 row 6, severity **medium**]. F28's H4 measured Jaccard **FULL 1.000 = CONTROL 1.000, effect +0.000** — for *file selection*, which is the only thing it measured. | AC-031, AC-329; ADR-0005 and ADR-0006 (both partially superseded by **ADR-0075**) |
| **9** | **One dispatch, one schema, per activity.** Sequential work gets a single agent; orchestration is reserved for the parallel project DAG and encapsulated review panels. *Forbids:* decomposing sequential work across agents to buy quality. | Sequential multi-agent decomposition **−39…−70%**, parallel **+80.9%**, error amplification **17.2×** independent vs **4.4×** centralised [preprint, arXiv 2512.08296, re-verified 2026-09-08 — a Google Research/DeepMind study, not "Google/MIT"; the cited 180-config / 4-benchmark figures are the original version, not the Apr 2026 v3's 260 configs / 6 benchmarks]; automatic multi-agent systems "consistently underperform CoT-SC despite being up to **10×** more expensive" across six frameworks [preprint, arXiv 2606.13003]. | ADR-0022, ADR-0026. AC-228 (the fan-out stagger) is **PROVISIONAL** — no fan-out spike ran; the vendor's mechanism is a capped hold-until-first-response-begins (code.claude.com/docs/en/workflows), not a per-sibling interval, and SM-114's A/B compares three arms (PIA-057) |
| **10** | **Gate duration is free, frequency is marginally costly, placement is a value decision.** Skipping is `0` — explicit and logged. *Forbids:* justifying or refusing a gate on its duration; pricing a gate without naming its arrival frequency. | **18,603 tokens** at both **65.2 min** and **49.7 h**, **one draw per duration point** [measured, F28 as corrected]; gate→verdict median **10.5 min** with **45%** over an hour, stage→verdict median **51.6 min**, p90 **~9 h**, max **226 h (9.4 days)** [measured, F18; one operator, one machine]; mid-graph resume lossless at n=3 with its cost against a stage gate **unmeasured** (ADR-0106) [measured, F38, n=3, PARTIAL]. | AC-038, AC-039, SM-008; ADR-0087, ADR-0106 |

It creates **29,680** tokens at share **0.000** at 66.8 min [F28, **n=1**] and **29,619** at 66.1 min [S-H1b, **n=1**, no finding number] — **two single draws, never a range** — where the default prompt costs a flat **18,603**, one draw per duration point [measured, F28 correction / S-H1b]. Every distribution finding in this programme is `[documented]` from *shipmates'* issue history — one project, one maintainer, one release series — and redkiln-rs has shipped no binary, so AC-480..AC-519's distribution half are borrowed-failure criteria and every measure there starts with "no baseline". **PROVISIONAL** per §0.1. | AC-480..AC-519 (distribution half), SM-145..SM-154; ADR-0011 (`rk-plugin-gen` is Phase 2) |

**The caution that principle 5 carries.** F38's mid-graph result is **n=3**, all three runs sat **inside** F10's ~1 h TTL (measured `gap_minutes` 0.9, 28.4, 29.1, notwithstanding the `0-delay` arm label), and there is **no within-experiment baseline** because all three no-kill controls are unrun — the comparison is only to F28 — and that comparison divided unlike counters (`output_tokens` over `cache_creation_input_tokens`, E-0087-1), so **the cost ordering is UNMEASURED and "mid-graph resume is cheaper than a stage gate" may not be written at all** (ADR-0106, superseding ADR-0087's cost clause; PIA-080). The label "design final" is blocked on the same unrun arms; see §0.1.

**The caution that principle 12 carries.** Every false-positive figure behind it measures false *rejection*. The mirror risk is unmeasured and documented as large — strong bug-free framing cuts vulnerability detection by **16–93 percentage points on 5 of 6 models** and shifts the whole classifier toward "clean", false positives collapsing with it [documented, arXiv 2603.18740 v2, fetched 2026-09-10]. Until the S-SUBTLE prior-approval framing arm runs, **no section may claim the review gate is guarded against false approval** (ADR-0101), and CLEAN is never a denominator.
