| Replay has never been measured | AC-006..AC-009 are the instrument that would prove the split; SM-009 has no baseline | Nightly replay of the last N runs against fakes at $0; a single divergence blocks the Phase-1 "deterministic resume across kill" exit criterion | kernel replay test suite · consumer: Phase-1 exit criteria (§10) | Yes — it is Phase-1 work, not a spike |
| Multi-step resume generalised from N=1 | AC-013 and SM-002 assert over N committed steps; F38 recorded exactly one | AC-013 marked PROVISIONAL; the cost ordering against a stage gate is **UNMEASURED** and "cheaper than a stage gate" may not be stated at all (ADR-0106, superseding ADR-0087's cost clause) | **P8-B** — the 65-minute and 24-hour arms and all three controls, unrun, on `cache_creation_input_tokens` beside `total_cost_usd` | No — gates the label, not the build |
| `system/api_retry` payload — **CAPTURED, the check is discharged** | AC-026 / AC-232 count inner attempts against the activity budget; the payload is captured on **139 rows across 12 result files, `spikes/results/e3.jsonl` included** [measured, PIA-068] — not a census entry with assumed fields | Both criteria cite `e3.jsonl` (and p7/s-var) as the captured fixture and drop PROVISIONAL on that ground; the per-row attempt distribution is recorded beside §0.3's C1, which becomes decidable by measurement [amended 2026-09-11, PIA-068] | **S-RETRY** keeps the throttle-margin arm and gains exhaustion-on-result (AC-523); consumers AC-027–AC-035, SM-003, SM-112, `rk-adapter-claude` (§10.4) · consumer: the kernel budget layer | No |
| `K`, `N`, backoff base/cap, the 2-tick margin, per-item appetite | Five numbers with no measured value anywhere in the corpus | `K`, `N` and the appetite declared in the pack and refused as code defaults; the backoff base 2 s and cap 5 min are kernel policy constants (AC-029); the 2-tick margin is `margin_ticks ≥ 2`, refused below it before any dispatch (AC-377, ADR-0110); the criteria verify conformance to the declared envelope, not that the envelope is right (PIA-055) | none registered — a value decision the corpus does not settle · consumer: pack authoring (Step 7e) | No |
| Rust 1.97/1.98 dyn-async-trait stabilisation — **READ, negative through 1.98.1 (2026-09-10)** | ADR-0076's working assumption was search-only until R-ASYNC's read; it now rests on the 1.97.0 and 1.98.0 release notes and tracking issue #133119 | The decision is correct under either answer; the check re-runs as a release-notes plus tracking-issue read at every toolchain-pin bump | **R-ASYNC** DONE (§10.5) · consumer: the toolchain-pin bump review, ADR-0102's 1.98.1 pin | No |
| Second-harness quality unmeasured | A Codex-sourced result may not close a gate | Gate-eligible adapter list is **data with exactly one entry** in Phase 1, checkable today with no Codex adapter present (AC-239, AC-496) | **S-CODEX**, registered 2026-09-11 in `spikes/METHOD-S-CODEX.md` — 53 dispatches required, 76 with the saturated S-SILENT check, cost unpriced (not the 46 first budgeted) — unrun, and absent from the brief's own unrun list | No |

---

## 4. Packs, the catalog, and selection — semantics decided, source blocked

### 4.1 The block, first

**The selection engine's context-vector source is BLOCKED on two independent axes.** The **LLM-emitted** vector is blocked by **ADR-0079, status `superseded` — superseded in full by ADR-0090 (accepted) at runbook Step 4.4**. (Corrected 2026-09-15, and this is the correction every other ADR-0079 status citation in this document points at. This sentence read *"**ADR-0079, status `proposed`** — the only non-accepted record in the set other than the full supersessions"*. Both halves are false: ADR-0079's frontmatter reads `status: superseded` with `superseded_by: ["0090"]`, so it is itself one of the full supersessions and no record in the set is left non-accepted. The correction is made **in place and dated, not by an erratum** — **DECISIONS-2026-09-15 D15-2**, which withdraws `E-0079-2` as an *instrument* while its obligation still lands: ADR-0090:108-110 records that the status change and the back-link are the **only** edits made to ADR-0079 and that its body is deliberately untouched, so there is no frozen line for an erratum to correct. **What this correction does not settle:** whether the block itself still stands. ADR-0090 decision 2 states that after its registry work no facet declares `source = "llm"` and AC-071 *"has nothing left to fail closed on"*; nothing in front of this amendment resolves §0.1's **BLOCKED** row against that, so the row's state is left exactly as written and the residual is named here rather than decided.) The **derived** vector, which AC-071's gate never reached because that gate keys on `source = "llm"` alone, is blocked by **ADR-0089, accepted 2026-09-09**, on measurement that postdates the brief. Nothing in §4 resolves either, and nothing later in this document may. The two are not the same question and may not be collapsed: F39 measures a derived vector and narrows ADR-0079's options without deciding among them.

| What P1 measured | Reading | Tag |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Modal `R(stage, ctx)` identical across ten draws | **5/20, 3/20, 2/20 and 2/20** items against an **80%** pre-registered line — the kill fires **4 of 4** | [measured, F36; 400 dispatches, 0 void, $21.37, one corpus pin] |
| The prescribed anchor-rewrite repair | Did not work | [measured, F36] |
| `external_dep` | Collapsed to a constant — **200/200 false**; an emitted value carrying no information | [measured, F36] |
| Three axes with **byte-identical** anchors | Drifted up to **−0.168** — larger than the effect the `alpha < 0.6` rule exists to detect, so **that rule is not a decidable predicate on this corpus** | [measured, F36] |
| `unknown` rates | novelty **46.0%**, size **39.5%**, blast_radius **31.0%**, criticality **27.0%** | [measured, F36, n=200 draws] |
| Arm D precision / recall | **SCORED 2026-09-09**, against 140 truth cells at pin `47faec9f` under METHOD-P amendment 9 — all 140 scored after the panel-file key `resolve-open-questions` → `resolve-open-question` was fixed and the scorer made to fail closed on a truth cell with no engine record; the 2026-09-09 run had scored 136 (ERRATA E-0089-2). **No facet clears §1.4's 0.95 bar**, and the sub-0.95 clause fires on five: `ui` **1.00** [0.65, 1.00] recall 0.78 (tp 7, fp 0, fn 2, tn 6, excl 4) — the point clears, **the interval does not**; `toolchain` 0.73 [0.48, 0.89] recall 0.79 (tp 11, fp 4, fn 3); `change` 0.72 [0.54, 0.85] recall 0.84 (tp 21, fp 8, fn 4); `binary_change` 0.60 [0.23, 0.88] recall 1.00 (tp 3, fp 2, fn 0, tn 15); `flag_exists` 0.00 [0.00, 0.66] (tp 0, fp 2, tn 13, excl 4); **`auth` 0.00 [0.00, 0.26]** (tp 0, **fp 11**, tn 4, excl 4) as first scored — **0/12 [0.00, 0.24]** after the key fix; `data_migration` **NOT MEASURABLE** — 0 positives on 20 items across all three sources, precision undefined, a perfect method scores identically, **the criterion has no denominator** (the expected count at a ≈ 1.7% base rate of schema-changing revisions, Qiu, Li & Su ESEC/FSE 2013 — the n-sizing input for a second corpus) | [measured, F39; 140 cells, all scored, **$0** model cost, **PANEL-ADJUDICATED**, never human-panel] |
| The 0.95 bar itself, at this n | **NOT REACHABLE for any facet however good.** `ui` is 7/7 and its lower bound is 0.65; **73** all-correct positives are needed for a lower bound above 0.95 at the registered two-sided z = 1.96 (92 with continuity correction; 72 Clopper–Pearson — "roughly 60" was the first statement, E-0089-2). A 20-item corpus cannot pass this criterion — the same class of defect as P1's alpha rule (F36) and P7's criterion 1 (F37): a pre-registered bar the registered sample size cannot decide. This is a **result about the pre-registration**, carried in F39's Limits; it is not an amendment to METHOD-P | [measured, F39, Limits 1] |
| Why `auth` scores zero | Not a threshold miss and not a tuning parameter. The rule is an **unanchored, case-insensitive regex over paths and diff lines** and it marks `auth = true` on **14 of 20 items**. `token` fired 44 times, **36 inside a design token**; `auth` 43 times, **40 inside *authored* / *author* / *authors* / *authoring* / *report-md-unauthored* / *only-the-author-can-advance***; `oauth` 8 times, **all 8 inside *coauthor*** (`oauth` sits inside `c-oauth-or`); `session` 8, six a *work* session; `role` 2, both an ARIA attribute. **The failure is correlated, not random:** the colliding vocabulary is the vocabulary of the instrument under test | [measured, F39 §2] |
| Absence of evidence, emitted as a confident boolean | **4 of the 20 items changed no code whatsoever** — every changed file is `.bklg/` bookkeeping or telemetry. The panel returned `unknown` on **all 16** of their semantic-facet cells; the script emitted a confident boolean on every one. That is the `excl = 4` column. `unknown` is **excluded from scoring and never counted against the script**; reporting those 16 cells as agreement would have raised every semantic precision figure | [measured, F39 §4, Limit 6] |
| How the truth set was built | 140 cells (all scored after the panel-file key fix; the 2026-09-09 run scored 136 — E-0089-2; the corrected breakdown is F39's appended table's): **60** deterministic oracle (git metadata, independently implemented), **39** unanimous panel, **9** unanimous panel where the adversarial flag was mechanically moot, **32 human adjudication** — Ryan, the panel's and the script's positions revealed only after he answered; the `blind_attestation:` line (who, what was not opened, when) was written by Ryan on 2026-09-11, and the adjudication surface was a claude.ai Artifact page outside the repository (DECISIONS-2026-09-11 #13). Ryan's verdicts: **8 true, 24 false, 0 unknown, and 15 of 32 differ from the script**. The panel labelled 80 semantic cells: 39 accepted unanimous (23 decided, 16 honest `unknown`), 41 flagged | [measured, F39 §5] |

The composition mechanism is the point, not any single axis: per-facet alpha looks healthy on 4 of 8 axes while `R` almost never repeats, because four axes each flipping on a minority of draws compose into an unstable required set, and `unknown` is treated as `high` by position, so an axis oscillating `unknown ↔ low` moves `R` the full band width [measured mechanism, F36 / brief C.9].

Three external results corroborate the instability and rule out the cheap repair:

| Source | What it establishes | Tag |
| --- | --- | --- |
| *Same Input, Different Scores*, arXiv 2603.04417 (Feb 2026) — GPT-4o, GPT-4o-mini, Gemini-2.5-Flash, Claude-Haiku-4.5, Claude-Sonnet-4.5 | "Despite expectations of stability at temperature=0, substantial variability persisted across models"; temperature=0 had limited or inconsistent effect for the Anthropic models | [preprint] |
| *Introducing Background Temperature*, arXiv 2604.22411 (Apr 2026) | Measures **Claude-Sonnet-4 at T_bg = 0** — perfectly deterministic in their harness. That this places P1's instability on the **schema/prompt** side rather than the decoding side is **this design's inference**, not the paper's claim; neither preprint states a schema-induced mechanism | [preprint; the mechanism clause is inferred, F36 + ADR-0079 — PIA-020] |
| arXiv 2511.01176 (code-clone classification) | Asserts, as an **unmeasured premise** in its §5.2, that instability correlates with proximity to the decision boundary; what it *measures* is consistency over five identical submissions (GPT-4o > 90%, Llama 3.1 up to 80% inconsistent) and that temperature has a small influence on consistency — the latter is what supports "no temperature spike" | [preprint; the boundary relation is the paper's premise, not its result — PIA-020] |

**A temperature spike is explicitly not the answer** (research pass §4 rec. 21, fetched 2026-09-08). The one cheap diagnostic available against existing P1 data is arXiv 2511.01176's boundary-distance bucketing of the draws already recorded — it is carried in the spike register as **P1-BOUND**, a $0 sub-arm of P1-D (§10.4, §10.5), not here.

**What the block forbids, at full strength.** Phase 1 may not author a `require` row keyed on `criticality`, `novelty`, `size`, `blast_radius`, `market_facing`, `external_dep` or `has_repro` sourced from an LLM classification dispatch. `kind` is out of scope of the block: it is human input (ADR-0030 rule 4). Three criteria carry halves of this block that the prose above argues from and that were previously pinned only through AC-434's display scope: **AC-069** and **AC-079** fix the band rule itself — `unknown` is treated as `high` **by position**, so an axis oscillating `unknown ↔ low` moves `R` the full band width, which is the composition mechanism, not a display artefact; **AC-070** refuses a `require` row fed by a facet whose **measured distribution is degenerate** — F36's `external_dep` at 200/200 false is the named instance, and §4.5 pins the *registry* half of that through AC-072/AC-108 while AC-070 pins the *distribution* half; and **AC-074** is the mechanism §4.9's operator-load hedge and §10.2's "every ordinal axis is `source = human`" prohibition actually rest on — **a human override is stored as a decision-memory row and replayed on identical input**, so the human-sourced regime is not a per-run re-ask. **AC-073** keeps booleans and ordinals in separate registries so a boolean cannot be ranked. **The panel clause is discharged; the block is not.** F39 scored arm D on 2026-09-09, which satisfies **AC-071's fourth clause and no other** — clauses 2, 3 and 5 were already met by arm L's own record, 20 items × 10 draws at a pin, and that record fails clause 1: modal required-set identity **0.25 / 0.15 / 0.10 / 0.10** against the 0.80 line [measured, F36]. So **every ordinal axis stays `source = "human"`** — supplied or confirmed at the triage gate — and the engine refuses an LLM-emitted value for it. That prohibition is not a prose hedge: it is machine-checked by **AC-071**, whose `redkiln lint --facet-stability` gate refuses pack load while any facet declares `source = "llm"` without a scored P1 record (identity ≥ 0.80, n ≥ 20 items, **≥ 10 draws per item** — AC-071's executable precondition reads `rec.draws_per_item >= 10`, and an earlier revision of this document wrote 5 — arm-D panel scored, corpus pinned).

**The parallel clause that formula was silent on, and that F39 forces.** "Every ordinal axis is `source = "human"`" says nothing whatever about `source = "derived"`, and that silence is exactly the gap: AC-071 keys on `source = "llm"`, so the semantic facets sat **outside the block entirely and are the ones measurably wrong**. Under **ADR-0089**, accepted 2026-09-09: **no facet may declare `source = "derived"` without a named `deriver` and a record for that deriver** — six facets at the pin, each with a record, NOT MEASURABLE ones included, while `stage`/`rigor`/`role`/`cost` are `source = "kernel"` and `surfaces` is `source = "declared"`, neither gated (**ADR-0093**, partially superseding 0089; the closed `source` set is `human \| llm \| derived \| kernel \| declared`) — and a facet whose only computation is a lexical scan of free text does not satisfy PC8 rule 2 (ADR-0030) for `require`-row purposes — rule 2's prohibition is unchanged in force and strengthened in content. The repo-wide `auth` boolean is **withdrawn** and replaced by a **set-valued `surfaces` facet** over a checked-in `surfaces.toml` path-glob map, a fact that is the intersection of the changeset with a declared boundary rather than a lexical hit; set-valued is precedented by `toolchain` and `change`, though it is a **third registry shape** the loader must recognise explicitly rather than by omission. Provenance — **DECLARED > DERIVED > PROPOSED > ASKED** — is carried on the value so a `require` row may demand a minimum, and each activity's `applicable_if` owns its own evidence question (ADR-0024). `surfaces` is **unscored until S-FACET runs**: it may be authored and linted, and it may not key a `require` row on ADR-0089's strength alone. And **boolean facets become tri-valued — absence of evidence is `null`, never `false` — routed to AC-079's existing `gate_opened { reason: unknown_facet }`**, the route the booleans could not reach because their declared domain is `[true, false]`. ADR-0067's `recent_deploy` already emits `null` under rule `R-recent-deploy-feed-absent`, so the pattern is **generalised, not invented**; abstention is reported separately from error, and a `null` cell is never counted as a false negative. **ADR-0089 does not touch ADR-0079** and does not decide source asymmetry.

**AC-076** carries the panel itself as a human gate node, explicitly not machine-decidable and with no threshold stated. Its warrant has changed and the criterion has not: arm D is no longer uncomputed, but METHOD-P §1.4's precision line is **not reachable at n = 20** (F39, Limit 1), so there is still no pre-registered precision/recall threshold this document can cite as machine-decidable.

**Three adjacent unknowns, named rather than glossed.** **P2 is half-run: its perturbation half is SCORED and its advisory half is not.** The perturbation half was scored on 2026-09-10 over **41 `(case, downstream)` rows** across 12 perturbation cases at pin `47faec9f`, at **$0** model cost, on labels that are **blind and timestamp-verified** rather than attested — `labelled_at` 2026-09-10T15:58:08Z precedes the engine's earliest record at 16:01:12Z, checked by the scorer's own guard, and P2 is untouched by METHOD-P amendment 8, which makes it the only arm here whose blindness is *checked*. **0 of 41 rows are "should re-run"** — 11 of the 12 cases changed nothing but YAML frontmatter (`stage`, `status`, `updated`, `gate_open`) and the twelfth (P01) changed **one body line** — a single rewritten line, which the unified diff shows as a `-`/`+` pair of wording — so **recall has no denominator and METHOD-P §2.4's criterion 1 (recall half) and criterion 2 are NOT ANSWERABLE**, while criterion 1's **precision half fires** at 0.00 [0.00, 0.26] over 11 emitted positives. Precision is not the honest statistic on a label set with no positive class; **specificity is — coarse 0.73** [0.58, 0.84] against **fine 0.55** [0.38, 0.70], intervals overlapping, with the paired comparison over the 33 rows both scopes scored separating them instead: 5 `Stale` coarse against 15 fine, **10 rows flipping `Present` → `Stale` and none back**. **The remedy the firing criterion prescribes — fine scope mandatory for `L`-class — is therefore contraindicated by the measurement that fires it** [measured, F40]. Criterion 3, the **advisory half (A01–A06), is still UNRUN**, behind DECISIONS-2026-09-09 #15's `human_authored` remap. So the `stale_policy` setting of step 3 is **not** taken from P2 — it is taken by explicit decision on 2026-09-11 (`rematerialise`, coarse scope, every class, recorded per class with its warrant at Step 7e; DECISIONS-2026-09-11 #4): **AC-068**'s pass condition inverts to that decision rule, SM-021 still has no target, and §0.1 carries the evidence as a BLOCKED row discharged by **P2-B** and by the advisory half. And **`ChangeSummary`** is one of the five toolchain-keyed artifact kinds §4.3 expands per toolchain, but its **type design is blocked on S-BIN, registered and unrun** [S-BIN reported (s8i) 2026-10-14: run 2026-10-14, ten records valid at corpus `835b122`; F43 and ADR-0199, both proposed *(dated 2026-10-15, `s8i` close, D21-154: F43 and ADR-0199 accepted)*, fix the `Binary` arm's four fields and route an item carrying a `Binary` path to the human gate] — so no criterion in this area constrains its content (packs-catalog gap 3). And **source asymmetry is a separate, untaken decision, registered as an arm of S-FACET and decided nowhere in this document**: `commits` beats `docs` on every facet F39 could measure — `toolchain` **1.00** [0.70, 1.00] against **0.50** [0.22, 0.78], `change` **0.80** [0.61, 0.91] against **0.50** [0.24, 0.76], `ui` 1.00 at recall 0.67 against 1.00 at recall 0.22, and `binary_change` **0.60 at recall 1.00 against no positives at all** from `docs` (recall 0.00, 3 missed), because a card names a path but carries no binary marker, so the facet is unreachable from prose by construction — and **`union` is worse than `commits` alone** on `toolchain` (0.73) and on `change` (0.72), because unioning a precise source with an imprecise one imports the imprecision [measured, F39 §3]. Which source the engine trusts is independent of the regex question and is not settled here.

**What is *not* blocked.** The engine's semantics are decided and buildable now: purity, the seven steps, rank, tie-is-a-validation-error, unsatisfiable-is-a-typed-gate, deviation-never-edits-`R`, replay, and the whole lint surface. Everything from §4.2 to §4.7 is written for a **given** context vector and is indifferent to where that vector came from. **AC-113** states exactly that scope, and is marked PROVISIONAL for the same reason.

### 4.2 Two pack axes, and the hard line

Packs are TOML, shallow, validated in Rust with spans, on two axes. One axis owns structure; the other owns bindings and contributions through named extension points — the resolution every build system that hit this tension converged on (Nx targets vs executors, Tekton Pipelines vs Tasks, Buildpacks' ordered detect-and-contribute, GitHub reusable workflows vs composite actions) [documented, proposal §4.2.1].

| Layer | Owns | Never owns | Enforced by |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Process pack** | stages, activities, gates, rigor per classification; **roles** (`implementer`, `reviewer`, `investigator`, `spec-author`); tier policy per role; **slots** (named extension points in a stage) | any agent card; any toolchain command | ADR-0014 [documented] |
| **Agent card** | identity, heuristics, return format, capabilities; which role(s) it *fulfils*; an optional *selector* | orchestration; model choice — the tier lives on the role | AC-118 (a card setting `model` fails `doctor`); ADR-0014 |
| **Technology pack** | toolchain adapter config; **skills** attached by rule; **specialised cards** fulfilling a role under a selector; **slot fillers** inserted at declared slots | new stages; edits to process activities; rubric rows; `require` rows | AC-080, AC-081, AC-082; ADR-0014, ADR-0029 [documented] |

**The hard line: a technology pack may bind and contribute at slots; it may never restructure.** A `[stage.*]` table in a technology pack, a collision with a process pack's activity id, and a rubric row or acceptance requirement contributed by a technology pack are each refused at load as a configuration error naming the file and span — AC-080, AC-081, AC-082 respectively, all configuration-time, all zero-dispatch (AC-098). When an ecosystem genuinely needs a different shape — a cook/bake stage that is not a pre-test slot — that is a **process-pack variant** (`story-ue`) selected at triage or by config, and the run record names the variant and the source of that selection, both non-null (AC-085), so the shape change is attributable to one named choice rather than to two packs silently merging. AC-085 is honestly a **schema-presence** criterion: it proves the choice was recorded, never that it was right.

### 4.3 The binder — and equal specificity is a configuration error

The binder is deterministic Rust, run at plan time and recorded in the event log [documented, proposal §4.2.1; ADR-0014].

| Binder rule | Behaviour | Criterion |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1. Role → candidate cards → most specific selector wins — **specificity is subsumption** over the closed selector key set: selector A is more specific than B iff every key B constrains A constrains to a value at least as narrow, and at least one strictly; two selectors neither of which subsumes the other are **incomparable** and resolve as `AmbiguousRole` [amended 2026-09-11, PIA-046] | Two candidates at **equal or incomparable specificity** for the item at hand are a **configuration error**, never a guess: resolution fails naming both cards, and the binder never orders them by file order, load order or name | **AC-087**; a property test that shuffling pack load order never turns an ambiguous case into a resolved one |
| 1b. No card matches a (role, toolchain) pair | `doctor` reports an unresolved role and exits non-zero, before any run is planned | **AC-086** |
| 1c. Mixed-toolchain item | Resolves through **toolchain-keyed artifact kinds**, one card per keyed kind; **no specificity tie-break is consulted for the mixed case** | **AC-088**; ADR-0073 (partially supersedes ADR-0029) |
| 2. Skills attach by rule (toolchain × role × stage × file-glob) and compile into the card's stable prefix | The skill set is therefore inside the card digest and inside the activity memo key: editing a skill invalidates the memo for every activity bound to that card | **AC-091**. F16 measured cache-read share (no-commit 1.000 · commit-each 0.689 · `--system-prompt` 1.000) [measured, F16, 50 dispatches]; the step from "a stable prefix pays once" to "the card must be in the digest" is [inferred], not measured |
| 3. Slot fillers insert in declaration order — **cross-pack order is the enabled-pack list in `.redkiln/config.toml`, then within-pack declaration order** [amended 2026-09-11, PIA-046] | A filler naming an undeclared slot is a configuration error; two packs filling one slot yield **both** fillers, in that order, with byte-identical resolution records | **AC-083**, **AC-084** |
| 4. The resolution is written to the run record | Card, matched selector, attached skills, inserted fillers, tier — five fields, so "why did this run with that card" is answerable from recorded fields alone | **AC-090**, falsifiable by running `redkiln explain` **with the pack directory renamed away** |

**AC-087 and AC-088 must not be one code path.** Specificity still ranks selectors for the single-toolchain case; the anti-`specificity_tiebreak` lint of AC-088 must be scoped to the mixed case or it breaks AC-087. ADR-0073's stated measured cause — a mixed Node+UE item silently receiving the UE change summary only — is **`spikes/F35-DRAFT.md` §1.3** [measured, F35 §1.3]: the condensed brief's F35 ledger row carries only the aggregate rates, which is why an earlier revision of this paragraph read the citation as absent and called the rule evidence-thin; that reading is withdrawn (the primary spike record governs the brief, ADR-0092 decision 2.4) [amended 2026-09-11, PIA-075]. Its most consequential instance (`ChangeSummary`) is in any case blocked on S-BIN.

Binder determinism is asserted **cross-machine** and instrumented as such: a same-process double-run cannot detect path-order, locale or hash-seed dependence, so **AC-089** is a two-OS CI matrix comparing resolution digests as build artifacts. AC-089 is determinism **given a fixed facet vector**; the stability of an LLM-emitted vector is the separate, blocked question of §4.1.

### 4.4 Selection is a pure function of four digests, in seven steps

`select()` is a function of `(pack digest, catalog digest, context digest, artifact digests)` and of nothing else [documented, catalog §2.5; ADR-0024].

| Step | What it does | The invariant that makes it safe | Criterion |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 · **Rigor** | Per stage from `policy.<class>` or an explicit item-frontmatter override (logged). `forbidden` is a **logged skip**, not an absence. **`discouraged` is reserved**: its semantics (baseline runs, `require` rows fire, the run record carries an advisory) are not defined in Phase 1, and a lint rejects it in any pack until a record defines it [amended 2026-09-11, PIA-046] | Rigor is ordinal and admits comparison only; `forbidden` means nothing in that stage runs, **including via another activity's `requires`** | AC-050; AC-051 (load), **AC-077** (runtime non-dispatch) |
| 2 · **Required set** | `R(stage, ctx)` = `baseline` ∪ artifacts of every `require` row whose `when` holds | Every predicate resolves to artifact presence, a digest comparison, a **registered facet value** — an ordinal, a **set** membership (`toolchain` and `change` today, `surfaces` under ADR-0089), or a boolean's `true` / `false` / **`null`** — or a typed verdict; a judgement predicate ("stakeholders in agreement") fails load with `JudgementPredicate`. **A boolean facet with no evidence is `null`, never `false`** (ADR-0089): absent inputs are already `null` (ADR-0032) and the arithmetic is total under **ADR-0103**: `null == null` is true, `null == v` for non-null `v` is `null` (as are `!=` and `in`), `not null` is `null`, `and`/`or` are three-valued, **`all` and `none` over `null` or an empty list are true while `any` is false** (ADR-0032's "vacuously true" withdrawn for `any`), ordering against `null` stays a load-time lint error unless guarded by the one narrowing pattern `x != null and <ordering on x>` at the same conjunction level (ADR-0069). A predicate whose top-level value is `null` is the **abstention case**: at selection it emits `gate_opened { reason: unknown_facet, axis }` (AC-079), counted once per read facet — **never `Unsatisfiable`, which is reserved for step 4 over predicates that evaluated to `false`** — and escalates to the human gate rather than resolving silently. Every `require` row over a boolean facet must handle `null` explicitly, and one that does not is a lint error rather than a silent `false`; `any` over absent evidence must not pass an activity (a must-fail fixture) [amended 2026-09-11, PIA-048] | AC-065, AC-079; ADR-0033, ADR-0050, **ADR-0089**, **ADR-0103** |
| 3 · **Short-circuit** | Per artifact: `Present` / `Stale` / `Missing`, decided on `derived_from` digests. **`stale_policy`'s schema** [amended 2026-09-11, PIA-044]: the TOML site is `policy.<cost_class>.stale_policy`, the enum is `rematerialise \| gate`, and `L`-class means the `cost_class` of the artifact kind's registered producer in the requiring stage, resolved before step 3 classifies; the `derived_from` scope is `coarse` (the declared map) or `fine` (the reconstruction), fine never mandatory. **The Phase-1 value is taken by decision, not default** (DECISIONS-2026-09-11 #4): `rematerialise` with coarse scope for every class, recorded per class with its warrant at Step 7e; Checkpoint 8 asserts no value was defaulted from P2 | A missing `derived_from` entry is `Stale`, never assumed fresh. A **vouched root** (human-supplied, `derived_from = []`) stays `Present`; drift against it is an **advisory** that can never promote itself to a block, emitted when an artifact of a kind listed in the registered producer's `requires` for the root's kind, in the same stage (ADR-0025), changes digest — a same-stage artifact outside that edge set changing emits nothing (AC-067's edge set; PIA-044); the positive half — exactly one `drift_advisory { root, upstream, old_digest, new_digest }` appended and carried on the next `gate_opened` record — is **AC-525** (ADR-0025 made checkable, PIA-029) | AC-066, AC-067, AC-068; ADR-0025. **This mechanism now has a measured false-alarm rate.** Over 41 `(case, downstream)` rows at pin `47faec9f`, specificity is **0.73** [0.58, 0.84] on the **declared** (coarse) map and **0.55** [0.38, 0.70] on the **reconstructed** (fine) one, which abstains on **8 of 41 = 19.5%** [0.10, 0.34]. Two scope clauses hold that in place: the corpus carries **no positive labels at all**, so nothing there measures *detection* — precision is 0.00 [0.00, 0.26] by construction and recall is **not computable** — and F40 scored a **reconstruction** of `derived_from` (the old redkiln never recorded one), not the recorded value this step is specified against [measured, F40]. `stale_policy` itself is **PROVISIONAL (AC-068) and BLOCKED in §0.1**: P2's perturbation half is scored, its advisory half is unrun, two of its three criteria are **not answerable**, and the remedy the one that fires prescribes — fine scope for `L`-class — is contraindicated by the fine scope being the worse of the two here |
| 4 · **Producer** | Candidates = activities with the kind in `provides`, `applicable_if` true (evaluated over the item's context vector at selection, never re-evaluated inside the activity — ADR-0103 decision 6), **`min_rigor ≤ r ≤ max_rigor`** where an absent ceiling is no upper bound (ADR-0056; `bisect.toml`'s `max_rigor = "recommended"` is the shipped instance, ADR-0093 decision 5) [amended 2026-09-11, PIA-054], `stage` equal to the requiring stage; **lowest `method_rank` wins**; recurse on the producer's `requires` | **A tie is a pack validation error, never a runtime choice** (SHOP2 ordered methods). No candidate ⇒ `Unsatisfiable { kind, failed_predicates }` ⇒ gate — over predicates that evaluated to `false`, never over a `null` (ADR-0103 decision 5) | AC-046, **AC-047**, AC-052, **AC-054** |
| 5 · **Validate** | Acyclic; every `requires` bound; every element of `R` reachable; `Σ cost_class` within the item's appetite; `excludes` respected. Emits `dag_assembled { inputs_digest, nodes, edges, rows_fired, skipped }` | The engine **never trims, defers or drops** a member of `R` to fit the appetite — it gates, carrying the DAG, the appetite and the overage | AC-058, **AC-078** (runtime acyclicity), AC-059/AC-060 (load-time cycle and `excludes` symmetry) |
| 6 · **Execute** | Topological order; antichains parallel where §7 allows | The engine's own invariant is that execution **cannot change `R`** — step 7 is the only channel, and it re-enters at step 2 | **No criterion in the thirteen area files covers step 6.** §7.7 states four parallelism rules and names exactly one criterion (**AC-388**, the stagger); the sequential-within-a-tree, one-worktree-per-antichain-member and merge-queue rules are pinned by **AC-189** and **AC-196**–**AC-198** in the AC register's git-reconcile section (`acceptance-criteria.md` §6; §7.7 below), and **the execute step of the pure selection function itself is uncovered**. Recorded as a gap, not papered over |
| 7 · **Deviation** | An activity may return `RequestDeviation { target, kind, claimed_predicate, why }`. The engine re-evaluates the predicate against evidence: true ⇒ re-run 2–5 and log the delta; false ⇒ gate with the request and its evaluated operands attached | **The agent never edits `R`.** `RequiredSet` is constructible only inside the selection crate and implements no deserialisation, so no agent-returned value can reach it; a `kind` outside the activity's own `deviation_ok` is refused as a configuration error and never reaches predicate evaluation | AC-061, AC-062, AC-063, AC-064; ADR-0046 |

**Lowest rank wins; a tie is a validation error.** This is the one place the design refuses a defensible-looking fallback. AC-047 fails load with `RankTie { kind, context, activities }` before any activity is dispatched, and the shipped catalog's tie count must stay 0 as a golden assertion. Measured baseline: **rank ties 0** across the enumerated lattice [measured, F35, n=1 enumeration, one machine]. The stronger claim — that the engine exposes *no* runtime tie-break at all, no code path, no declaration-order fallback — was **cut from AC-047** as an absence claim over a crate that no named instrument decides; it survives as design intent in ADR-0056 only. Note also that once ties and cycles are build failures they cannot recur without CI failing, so those two zeroes carry no discriminating content (SM-014's own honesty note).

**Unsatisfiable produces a typed gate and nothing else.** No next-best rank, no relaxation of `min_rigor`, no substitute kind, no dropping the artifact from `R`; the producer-resolution function has no default arm (AC-054, a negative criterion). The gate record names the kind, every failed predicate, **the evaluated value of each facet that predicate read — which may itself be `null`, recorded as an abstention with the rule that produced it and never coerced to `false`** (ADR-0089; the route is AC-079's existing `gate_opened { reason: unknown_facet }`, extended to the boolean facets, and ADR-0067's `recent_deploy` under `R-recent-deploy-feed-absent` is its live precedent) — and the rigor in force, and the store's single write path refuses a record missing any of them (AC-055) — the shape F31 showed typed payloads actually get filled (schema conformance **52/52 = 100%**, `repro.command` **147/147**) [measured, F31].

**The reproducibility contract.** Any run that cannot be replayed to the same DAG has a **hidden input**, and the memo key is then **unsound** [documented, catalog §2.5; ADR-0024]. This is enforced in three places, not asserted:

- **AC-041** — the selection crate cannot read wall-clock time, environment variables, the filesystem, the network or a random source; such a call **fails to compile** rather than reading at runtime (`trybuild` compile-fail plus a dependency/import lint). The precedent is measured: a default `claude -p` silently inherited 12 MCP servers and ~280 tools, **56% of the cache write**, and an ignored isolation flag looks identical to an honoured one [measured, F8].
- **AC-042** — `redkiln replay --run <id>` re-derives the DAG from the logged inputs alone and reports `HiddenInput { run_id, first_divergent_node }` on any mismatch, never a warning. **This is a first measurement.** F19's stated limit is that **the FSM was never replayed** — 174/174 suspensions were lossless, but "same next action" is unproven [measured, F19, one operator]. SM-013 records **no baseline** and reports non-identical runs as a count, never as a percentage rounded to 100%.
- **AC-043** — a memo entry minted by a run whose replay assertion failed is refused and re-materialised (`memo_evicted { reason: unsound_replay }`); one served hit from a non-replayable run is a correctness defect, not a rate to manage (SM-020). The memo key admits no construction that omits any of the four digests (AC-045).

Selection spends **$0.00** in model dispatches, always — the selection crate does not contain the `Harness` port or any adapter that can dispatch a model (AC-044), cross-checked at runtime by SM-019. Any model spend attributed to selection falsifies purity and invalidates every replay claim in this area.

### 4.5 The catalog v0

| Element | v0 shape | Source |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Activities | **102** in the lattice corpus over **12** packs; **90** authored in the P6 pass, against a curated **~95**-activity v0 ceiling with the remaining raw rows in an **unshipped extended file** | 102 / 12 / 90 are [measured, F35/F34]. **The ~95 ceiling is not measured**: ADR-0027's own consequences section records ~95/~170 as its approximate split of the 267 raw sourced activities, with no spike or count behind it — [documented, ADR-0027], planning estimate |
| Stages | Twelve, from `triage` (always run) to `closeout` (`finally`); kind-level constraints encoded as **`forbidden` rows**, not absence — incidents forbid discovery/spec/design, spikes forbid release | [documented, catalog §4] |
| Step kinds | **Five, closed**: `agent` (the only place a model runs), `tool` (a toolchain verb through the port), `check` (a deterministic predicate in Rust; cannot dispatch), `gate` (the kernel writes the mark and **exits 0**, resuming into the same step), `emit` (binds outputs to `provides`; exactly one per kind provided; terminal) | [documented, activity-graph schema §1]; ADR-0022 |
| Typed exits | **Three**: `Materialized { artifacts, evidence }`, `Blocked { reason, question }`, `RequestDeviation { … }`. A step failure with retries exhausted is `Blocked`, not an error the stage interprets. Nothing else leaks. **Alternative outcomes** (a canary that rolls back still "completes") are expressed by a final materialising step rather than a fourth exit — ADR-0041, **fully superseded by ADR-0064**, and the ADR README warns that anyone acting on 0041 must read 0064's Decision section for what survives; both ids are named here so the narrowing is reachable | [documented, schema §3]; ADR-0036 (evidence minting is closed per step kind), **ADR-0041 → ADR-0064** |
| Activity lifecycle | A coherence edit to a **shipped** activity bumps its **`version`** and enters the changelog — a shipped activity is never edited in place without both (**AC-111**, **AC-117**). The activity `version` (`rk-catalog`, ADR-0072, part of the memo key's `activity_id@version`) is a different field from an artifact type's **`schema_version`** (`rk-types`, AC-135, the golden snapshot's version) — the two are never conflated, and this document's earlier `schema_version` here was the register's `version` [amended 2026-09-11, PIA-036] | ADR-0072 [documented] |
| Registries | **Five**, one table per value with a date and a type: `artifacts.toml` (kinds, `source`, `keyed_by`), `facets.toml` (normative for the `toolchain` and `change` facets; under **ADR-0089** its `source` field gains a companion **`deriver`** naming the instrument, and it carries the set-valued `surfaces` facet whose values come from a checked-in `surfaces.toml` path-glob map; under **ADR-0093** `source` is the closed set `human \| llm \| derived \| kernel \| declared` — `stage`/`rigor`/`role`/`cost` are `kernel`, `surfaces` is `declared`, six facets are `derived`, and AC-072 recognises every token by name), `registry.toml` (toolchain verbs and feeds; a verb's `live = true` implies `memo = false` on every step using it), `questions.toml` (question ids, closed option sets, `attaches`), and the checklist registry | ADR-0057, ADR-0059, ADR-0061, ADR-0038/ADR-0063, ADR-0040 [documented] |

**Authoring is tractable, and the kill line was never approached.** Three authoring passes — **by one operator, not three independent people** — landed at median **56.5** (F32), **73** (F33, +29%), and **53** non-blank lines with sum 5,059 over 90 activities (F34), against a >150 kill: the shipped corpus clears by ~**2.8×** [measured, F32/F33/F34, n=3 passes, one operator]. First-run lint errors per file ran **4.7 → 1.3 → 0.11**, but the P6 author **read the lint before authoring**, which F34 states explicitly, so the 43× improvement is not attributable to the lint (SM-030). P9's prediction that amendments would fall was **falsified at n=3** (F33), so SM-034 is a **TRACKER with no target and no kill** and must not be cited downstream as assurance that the schema is stabilising. The lint's own precision is **13 real / 34 false = 27.7%** on one pass and it **accepted 5 guesses silently** across 27 ambiguities on another [measured, F32/F33] — so **AC-107** makes silent resolution impossible (`Err(Ambiguous)`, never a most-likely target), and SM-029 is baselined per instrument version — **13/13** (F32, post-fix), **2/4** (F33), **9/10** (F34) — with 27.7% recorded as the buggy-lint reading and not as a starting kill state [amended 2026-09-11, PIA-076].

**Where the schema amendments live, and which are in scope here.** ADR-0031–ADR-0073 are cited as a range in §0.1 and §1.1, and **twenty of them are SCHEMA-series amendments that this document does not reflect individually**. That is deliberate: they fix the *activity-graph schema's grammar*, whose home is `references/2026-09-07-activity-graph-schema.md`, and a design document that restated them would be duplicating a spec it does not own. They are named here in groups so a reader can find them rather than discover them by diffing:

- **Ref and predicate grammar** — absent optional inputs are `null`; `all`/`none` over `null` or an empty set are true while `any` is **false** (**ADR-0032**, its "vacuously true" withdrawn for `any` by **ADR-0103**, which also fixes precedence `not` > comparison > `and` > `or`, left-associative, the literal-versus-field rule by position, double-quoted string literals with single quotes as an alias, three-valued connectives, and the closed `on_false` form set — S-GRAMMAR is its check); a coalesce operator in refs (**ADR-0034**); parentheses and quoted facet-value literals in predicates (**ADR-0048**); quantifier bodies admit full comparisons (**ADR-0049**), and `len()` plus nesting (**ADR-0070**); ordering operators against `null` are lint errors while equality is defined (**ADR-0069**); `rigor` is a legal root in `applicable_if` (**ADR-0066**).
- **Structure and loops** — `for_each` sources and fan-out output projection are fixed syntactic forms (**ADR-0035**); `on_false` is a bounded table and never a bare scalar (**ADR-0044**); self-reference inside a loop binds the **previous** iteration (**ADR-0055**); a `gate` step inside a `for_each` is a lint error (**ADR-0068**); `After=<step>` shorthand is **deferred** (**ADR-0039**).
- **Tags, facets and artifacts** — same facet disjoins, distinct facets conjoin (**ADR-0050**); provided artifacts are unconditional at or above `min_rigor` (**ADR-0051**); optional artifact refs are legal and alternation inside `requires` is not (**ADR-0054**); boolean facets are `applicable_if`-only and tagging one is a lint error (**ADR-0060**, whose criterion **AC-109** is uncited by any section and is owed a home).
- **Lint rules** — rule 3 resolves paths against the step's **output** schema (**ADR-0052**); **R4b** requires an `on_false` target to be reachable at every rigor at which the check is active (**ADR-0065**).
- **Evidence and lifecycle** — empty evidence is legal **only** for activities with no agent step (**ADR-0071**); and **ADR-0072**, the one of the twenty that is *not* schema grammar, is reflected above as the shipped-activity version-and-changelog rule.

**Five registry facts constrain what may feed selection, today — three of registration and two of measurement.** `binary_change` is **measured but unregistered in `facets.toml`**, so it can feed no `require` row; `dynamism` is **pre-registered with no registry entry**; `recent_deploy` is **underivable at this pin**, notwithstanding ADR-0067 admitting it as a derived boolean [documented, brief §E contested list]. **AC-072** and **AC-108** make all three fail load if named in any `applicable_if`, `require.when` or `claimed_predicate` — an unregistered or underivable facet cannot silently become a selection input. **F39 adds the other two, and they are about the values rather than the entries.** First, `binary_change` is now also **measured wrong**: precision **0.60** [0.23, 0.88] at recall 1.00 over five emitted positives, so registering it would not on its own make it usable. Second — and this is the larger of the two — the facets that *are* registered and *are* derived are the ones carrying no signal: **`auth` scores 0.00 — every positive false (0/12 [0.00, 0.24] after the key fix; eleven of eleven before it) — and `flag_exists` 0.00 [0.00, 0.66]**, while `data_migration` emits **no positive at all** on this corpus and is **not measurable** rather than passing or failing [measured, F39; 140 cells, all scored, at pin `47faec9f`, $0, panel-adjudicated]. **ADR-0089** is the record, as **ADR-0093** narrows it: a facet may not declare `source = "derived"` without a named `deriver` and a record for it (six such facets; the kernel projections and the declared map are their own classes and are not gated); a lexical scan of free text does not satisfy PC8 rule 2 for `require`-row purposes; the repo-wide `auth` boolean is withdrawn for a set-valued `surfaces` facet over a checked-in `surfaces.toml`, **unscored until S-FACET runs**; and a derived boolean that cannot be computed is `null`, routed to AC-079's gate. The registry loader must recognise the set shape explicitly — `surfaces` is a third registry shape alongside boolean and ordinal, which ADR-0073 keeps in separate registries.

### 4.6 Completeness is CI, not a per-file lint

| Lattice reading | Pre-registered (8 pre-registered packs, pack-default reading) | 12 packs (pack-default) | After decisions 1–10 and Ryan's R3/R7 (12 packs; F35 §1.6 run9) | Pre-registered kill |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Unsatisfiable `(stage, ctx)` pairs | **23.5%** | **11.4%** | **0.3%** | >5% — **fires twice**, clears post-decision |
| Dead activities | **20.8%** (8 packs) | **14.9%** = 15/101 | **5.9%** = **6/102** | >20% — fires by **0.8 points** at 8 packs, clears at 12 |
| Rank ties | — | — | **0** | 0 tolerated |
| Hard cycles | — | — | **0** | 0 tolerated |
| Lattice runtime | — | **27 s** at run3 (101 activities, 16 bands, 12 packs) | unmeasured — the post-decision 81-band runs are under 2 min; a stamped timing is taken on the next run | — |

[measured, F35, n=1 enumeration, one machine, one catalog; the series is labelled by pack set and rigor mode per SM-027 — PIA-076]. **Both readings are reported**: the pre-registered reading fires the kill twice and the amended lattice clears all three criteria. **The 0.3% and 6/102 readings predate ADR-0089 and ADR-0093** (the facet re-classing, the `surfaces` facet, the `bisect` ceiling) and the Step 7e pack authoring; the lattice is re-run there and the new reading recorded as the AC-101/AC-102/SM-026/SM-027 baseline with its pack count and rigor mode [amended 2026-09-11, PIA-011]. Two consequences follow and are carried as criteria.

**First: a green per-file lint is never evidence of catalog completeness (AC-100).** The unsatisfiable cells arise from interaction between activities in **different files**, so CI runs the whole-catalog lattice independently of the per-file lint, with a regression fixture that is individually clean in every file and still fails the build. F35 records that **three quarters of first-run gaps were invisible to a per-file lint** and were found by the **27-second** whole-catalog run [documented, F35 §4 — the condensed brief's ledger row carries the aggregate rates, not that split; the 27 s is measured at run3]. AC-101 fails the build above the >5% line, AC-102 above the >20% dead line, AC-103 on any tie or cycle at zero tolerance, and AC-104/AC-116 enforce family totality and cap rank escalation at the highest total rigor — **ADR-0056 supplies the totality rule (its rule 26) and ADR-0058 the declared `family` catalog field with its three lint checks**; an earlier revision of this sentence had the two records' roles inverted, and AC-116 reads as a diagnostic on the failing report ("the failure names the highest total rigor r") rather than a cap [amended 2026-09-11, PIA-054].

**Second: a rate reported without its pack set is not a reading.** 8 packs versus 12 moved the dead-activity figure **5.9 points with the catalog unchanged** [measured, F35], so SM-027 reports the pack count alongside every reading and forbids comparing two readings taken at different pack counts. **The pack count is itself unaccepted.** The 12 are 8 pre-registered plus **four supplementary packs — `chore`, `tech-debt`, `security-vuln`, `docs` — which METHOD-P §3.2 has neither accepted nor struck** (F35 §3.1; HANDOFF "what to do next" item 3). Every 12-pack figure in this document — the 0.3% unsatisfiable and the 6/102 dead reading included — therefore rests on a pack set that is **one third provisional** and on a registry that predates ADR-0089/ADR-0093, and §0.1's DECIDED catalog row is decided on the *semantics*, not on that pack set. Discharge: **P6-PACKS** (§10.4/§10.5), one decision per supplementary pack, plus the v0 pack tagging step DECISIONS-2026-09-09 still lists as open work item (1) — which waits on ADR-0027's executed-once gate, unmet with 101 of 102 activities unrun (PIA-088); AC-057 writes both numbers into the lint report artifact keyed by catalog version. The two rate measures are, for now, **tripwires and not live signals**: 0.3% sits **16× inside** its 5% line (SM-026), and the verbosity median has never been at risk in three passes (SM-031). A green reading on either is not evidence that anything works.

**Cost of the check.** The whole-catalog lattice cost **$0** in model dispatches (P6, P9, P9b and the lattice work all did) and ran in **27 s** [measured, F35 / brief §D]. The 27 s is run3's — 101 activities, 16 bands, 12 packs — and the post-decision 81-band runs are unmeasured (under 2 min); SM-025's caveat is the band count, not the pack count (PIA-076), and how the lattice scales at 2× catalog size is **untested** — a hypothesis the measure exists to check, not a target.

### 4.7 Rigor is an ordinal deontic enum — and the ladder is not yet monotone

Rigor is `forbidden < discouraged < optional < recommended < mandatory` (ADR-0020, ADR-0023 — the same decision recorded in two homes, `related`, neither withdrawing from the other). It is **ordinal and never summed**: `Rigor` implements no `Add`, `Sum` or `Into<f64>`, and code that tries to combine rigor values arithmetically does not compile (AC-050, a `trybuild` compile-fail test). **The closed-policy risk is named where the decision is taken, not only where it is falsified.** A small enum that could not compose is a documented, dated, first-party failure shape: the Dagster RFC records exactly that outcome and its replacement by composable primitives [documented, 1P Dagster RFC on closed enums; research pass §4 rec 22]. The ordinal enum is adopted knowing that risk, and §10.6 registers "the catalog lint cannot be made monotone" as the result that would realise it. The justification for choosing an ordinal over a weighted score is external and general: **"rank reversal of one or another type occurs for virtually every MCDM method"**, TOPSIS and Simple Additive Weighting included, because weighted sums ignore that normalisation scale units differ across criteria — cite the general result, not an AHP-specific one, so "just use TOPSIS" is pre-answered [peer, Wang & Luo 2009 (S0895717708002860, covering SAW and TOPSIS) and Aires & Ferreira 2018, the sources arXiv 2312.03006 quotes the sentence from; García-Cascales & Lamata 2012 (S0895717711007850) is TOPSIS-only and retained for the TOPSIS-specific comparison, its full text unread — re-pinned 2026-09-11, PIA-098]. The local corroboration is that the judgement input to such a rubric is itself unstable: **verdict flaky on 2 of 4 items** over repeated draws while detection was flaky on **0 of 7** over 21 draws [measured, F27, n=2–3].

**The measured defect: the ladder is not monotone in internal quality.** Escalating to `mandatory` with `has_repro` currently selects `bisect`, which has **no adversary step and no policy step** — a higher rigor level that *drops* quality apparatus a lower one carried [measured mechanism, F37 / brief C.10]. This is cited as a **mechanism and never as a rate**: F37's primary criterion was **powerless at n=3**, every Wilson interval overlapping by construction, so no rate may be read off it. **The rung is repaired** (P7-CEIL, DECIDED 2026-09-11): `bisect.toml` gains `max_rigor = "recommended"`, so escalation to `mandatory` with `has_repro` selects a full activity and the family is monotone as shipped; adding the missing steps to `bisect` is the Phase-2 repair (ADR-0093 decision 5).

The fix is a new lint rule, not a catalog patch:

| Rule | Statement | Instrument |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Rank monotonicity** (ADR-0081; **AC-105**) | For any facet vector and any adjacent rigor levels *r* and *r+1*, the check fails if the graph selected at *r+1* omits a quality-apparatus step — adversary, policy, or a declared quality block — that the graph selected at *r* contains | A lattice CI monotonicity check across the **full facet × rigor cross-product**, plus a regression fixture reproducing the measured defect |

SM-035 records the baseline honestly: **at least one violation exists today**, the cross-product has **not** been enumerated, so the count is a **lower bound of 1, not a rate**; the target is 0, enforced as a build failure; and if violations cannot be removed without collapsing the ladder, then rigor is not a single ordered axis and the deontic enum needs a second dimension.

**Two things this area explicitly does not do.** It does not set the **quality shorthand default** — that is BLOCKED (ADR-0080; P7 powerless at n=3, F37; its re-run must change the criterion, ADR-0111), AC-105 constrains the ladder's *shape* and never its default level, and the forbidden predicate `findings.none(evidence in ["Reproduced", "MutationSurvived"])` may not be used as a terminal condition — an `rk-catalog` lint criterion with a red fixture carrying it is **AC-527** (ADR-0080 made checkable, PIA-029). And it quotes **no rigor-escalation cost multiplier**: F37's mandatory-arm ratio is **3.66×** (arm mean, n = 30) and **4.4×** (issue-77 r1, single run) — two denominators, reconciled (ADR-0106 clause 3) — and the mandatory arm was dropped at n=1 (packs-catalog gap 7). What *is* citable is a cost and a constant predicate: `adversary → policy` cost **2.64×** ($2.69 vs $1.02 per run), and its policy check was **false on 33 of 33** [measured, F37, 61 runs, 297 records, 0 void, $131.62]. **"Changed the root cause on 1 item in 10" is not citable and is excluded from that set**: it is one cell (`issue-81`, **0/3 against 1/3**) moved by a single draw of three — the powerless criterion's own output, not a result — and F37 §2 states that "nothing here excludes 0 and nothing excludes considerably more".

### 4.8 Decisions

| # | Decision | Alternatives rejected | Why | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 4-A | Two pack axes with a hard line: process packs own structure, technology packs bind and contribute at slots only | One pack type with an override algebra; technology packs allowed to append stages | Two packs silently merging makes the shape unattributable; the escape hatch is a named process-pack **variant** recorded in the run record | ADR-0014, ADR-0029 [documented]; AC-080..AC-085 |
| 4-B | Binder resolves role → card by most specific selector; **equal specificity is a configuration error** | Declaration order; file order; alphabetical; "most recently loaded wins" | Any implicit tiebreak makes the resolution a function of the filesystem, which AC-089's two-OS matrix would then fail | ADR-0014 [documented]; AC-087 |
| 4-C | Mixed-toolchain items resolve through **toolchain-keyed artifact kinds**, one instance per toolchain — the specificity tiebreak is withdrawn for the five keyed kinds | Keep "toolchain beats generic" for mixed items | The withdrawn rule silently delivered one toolchain's artifact for a two-toolchain item [measured, F35 §1.3 — `spikes/F35-DRAFT.md`, not the condensed brief's ledger row; the earlier "evidence-thin" note is withdrawn, PIA-075]; `ChangeSummary`'s type is blocked on S-BIN | ADR-0073 (partial supersession of ADR-0029) [documented]; F35 §1.3 [measured]; AC-088, AC-049 |
| 4-D | `select()` is a **pure function of four digests**, seven steps, with purity enforced at compile time and replay asserted per run | A "practical" purity by convention; reading the clock or the filesystem for convenience and documenting it | A hidden input makes the memo key unsound; F8 measured ambient environment entering a dispatch invisibly | ADR-0024 [documented]; AC-040..AC-045; F8, F19 [measured] |
| 4-E | Lowest `method_rank` wins; **a tie is a validation error at load**, never a runtime choice | Runtime tie-break by specificity, declaration order or cost | SHOP2's ordered-methods discipline; measured ties are 0, so the rule costs nothing today and forbids a whole class of silent divergence tomorrow | ADR-0056 [documented]; F35 [measured]; AC-047 |
| 4-F | Unsatisfiable ⇒ **typed gate**, with no fallback of any kind; over-appetite ⇒ gate, never a trimmed `R` | Next-best rank; relax `min_rigor`; substitute kind; drop the artifact | A silently reduced required set is the failure this whole model exists to prevent; the 0.3% residual must surface, not be papered over | ADR-0024, ADR-0056 [documented]; F35 [measured]; AC-054, AC-055, AC-058 |
| 4-G | Deviation is typed, per-activity, and **never edits `R`**; `RequiredSet` has no `Deserialize` impl | Let a warranted deviation write the required set directly; drop the channel as unused | Deviation was **offered 71 times and used 0** in 61 runs, so the channel must be closed by construction rather than by disuse — and the warranted fraction has an **empty denominator**, not a 0% | ADR-0046 [documented]; F37 [measured]; AC-061..AC-064, SM-017 |
| 4-H | Catalog completeness is **whole-catalog CI**, not a per-file lint; readings always carry their pack count | Per-file lint only; nightly-only lattice | Gaps arise across files; the check costs 27 s and $0, and the same rate moves 5.9 points on pack set alone | ADR-0027 [documented]; F35 [measured]; AC-100..AC-104, SM-027 |
| 4-I | Rigor is an **ordinal deontic enum**, never summed, and the ladder must be **monotone in internal quality** (new lint rule) | Weighted numeric stage weights (the proposal's 0.2/0.5/1.0); any MCDM weighted sum | Rank reversal is endemic to weighted MCDM methods; and the measured ladder already drops the quality block at `mandatory` | ADR-0020, ADR-0023, **ADR-0056** (the totality rule 26 behind AC-104), **ADR-0058** (families as a declared, lint-checked catalog field and its three lint checks), ADR-0081, ADR-0093 (the `bisect` ceiling); Wang & Luo 2009 S0895717708002860 / Aires & Ferreira 2018 via arXiv 2312.03006 [peer; S0895717711007850 TOPSIS-only]; **Dagster RFC on closed enums** [documented, 1P] — the closed-policy risk this decision accepts; F37 [measured mechanism] |
| 4-J | **No decision is taken on the LLM-emitted context-vector source.** ADR-0079 is not amended into a decision (corrected 2026-09-15; this read *"`proposed`"* — ADR-0079 is superseded in full by **ADR-0090**, and the correction is carried in place and dated, not by an erratum: see §4.1 and **DECISIONS-2026-09-15 D15-2**); every ordinal axis is `source = "human"` until AC-071's gate passes | Amend ADR-0079 into a decision; raise or lower temperature; rewrite anchors again; ship the alpha<0.6 rule as the predicate; **read F39 as deciding 0079** | The kill fired 4/4; the anchor-rewrite repair already failed at full n; the alpha rule is not decidable on this corpus; T_bg = 0 is measured for a Claude model (Sonnet-4, arXiv 2604.22411), which this design reads as pointing away from decoding noise — an inference the papers do not state (PIA-020). **F39 scores arm D and discharges AC-071's fourth clause and no other** — it measures a *derived* vector where 0079 asks about an *LLM-emitted* one, so it narrows the options without deciding among them; the block now stands on clause 1, identity **0.25 / 0.15 / 0.10 / 0.10** against 0.80 | ADR-0079 [documented]; F36, **F39** [measured]; arXiv 2603.04417 / 2604.22411 / 2511.01176 [preprint] |
| 4-K | **A decision *is* taken on the derived source, and it is not the same question.** A facet's computed fact must name its evidence source and provenance class (**DECLARED > DERIVED > PROPOSED > ASKED**); `facets.toml`'s `source` gains a companion **`deriver`** field and no facet may declare `source = "derived"` without a scored record for that deriver; the repo-wide `auth` boolean is **withdrawn** for a set-valued `surfaces` facet over a checked-in `surfaces.toml`; and **boolean facets become tri-valued, absence of evidence `null` and routed to AC-079's `gate_opened { reason: unknown_facet }`** | Anchor the regex and re-score it (retained as **S-FACET arm 2**, a control, never the answer); add keywords; put a confidence score on the rule; buy CodeQL, Semgrep or a call graph; train a classifier on this corpus; do nothing until S-FACET reports | `auth` at **0.00 — 0/12 with an upper bound of 0.24 after the key fix (eleven of eleven, 0.26, before it)** carries **no signal**, and anchoring removes the *coauthor* (8) and ARIA-`role` (2) hits while leaving *authored* (40) and design-`token` (36) untouched — a finding about the method, not a tuning parameter. Four empty-diff items produced **sixteen confident booleans** the panel would not answer. And "do nothing" was rejected because AC-071 gates `source = "llm"` only, so the registry stays **ungated while measurably wrong**; the abstention half is small, buildable, and independent of which deriver eventually wins | **ADR-0089** (partial supersession of ADR-0030, which stays `accepted`) [documented]; **F39** [measured]; `references/2026-09-09-change-classification.md` §2, §4, §6a [documented] |
